**الادّعاء:** The browser acceptance suite never completes a purchase as a guest — it renders the guest form and then clicks away from it to register — so the default COD buying path is proven only at the API level

**الأثر المزعوم:** The phase promised browser acceptance covering «a full purchase as guest and as customer». Buying without an account is the DEFAULT for every store (prisma/schema.prisma:192) and it is the path for the shopper this market is built around — the one who reaches the shop by phone and has no email. UI-01 walks right up to that form and then deliberately steps off it: it clicks `guest-login-link` and registers instead, so the guest name/phone/email fields, the guest address form, the `POST /checkout/guest` call, the session issued mid-flight, the guest-cart merge and the guest's landing on the order page have never been driven through a browser once. Everything below the fetch is covered by an API suite; everything the shopper actually touches is not. That is how the wrong-variant defect above and the permanent-spinner defect above both survive: the untested screen is the screen where both of them show.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/tests/phase9b.browser-acceptance.mjs:100-108 — `await pageA.getByRole("button", { name: "إتمام الطلب" }).click();` lands on checkout, then :101 `await pageA.getByTestId("guest-login-link").click();` immediately leaves the guest form for the register page, and :108-109 complete the purchase as a registered customer. The guest UI it walked past exists and is reachable: client/src/pages/StorefrontCheckoutPage.tsx:271 `data-testid="guest-checkout"`, :277-279 `guest-name` / `guest-phone` / `guest-email`, :196-204 the `storefrontApi.guestCheckout(...)` submit and `setCustomer(result.account)`. The only coverage of that endpoint is API-level: phase1-backend/tests/guest-checkout.integration.mjs:80, :104, :117, :133, :145, :169 all call `/store/${slug}/checkout/guest` over fetch. A grep of every .mjs suite for `guest-name` / `guest-checkout` testids returns nothing, and phase20a/phase20b browser suites do not touch it either. The scenario roster the suite prints at :240 lists UI-01…UI-12 with no guest-purchase scenario among them.

**العلاج المقترح:** Add a browser scenario that buys as a pure guest end to end: land on the product, add to cart, open checkout with no session, fill `guest-name`/`guest-phone` and the address form, submit, and assert the order-detail page renders the order number and that a subsequent `GET /account/orders` (on the session the server issued) lists exactly that one order.

### 11-15-63 · منخفضة · `.github/workflows/ci.yml:91`

**الادّعاء:** The promised lint stage of «lint → typecheck → build» does not exist: no lint script, no lint configuration anywhere, and prettier is wired only as a writer

**الأثر المزعوم:** One clause of the phase-11 pipeline is simply absent, while DEPLOYMENT.md:7 still describes CI as running «الفحص الساكن والبناء» as though a static-analysis stage existed beyond tsc. Nothing checks formatting on a pull request, so drift lands and reappears as unrelated noise in every later diff — the cheap kind of cost, but the clause is unkept, not met in a weaker form.

**الدليل المزعوم:** .github/workflows/ci.yml goes Install dependencies (:72-73) → Generate Prisma Client (:85-86) → Apply migrations (:88-89) → Typecheck frontend (:91-92) → Typecheck backend (:94-95) → Build (:97-98); no step between install and typecheck. package.json:6-158 defines no `lint` script: `check` (package.json:12) is `tsc --noEmit`, and `format` (package.json:13) is `prettier --write .` — a writer, never `--check`, and it is one of the scripts my sweep found unreferenced by ci.yml. A recursive search of the repository (excluding node_modules) for eslint.config.*, .eslintrc*, biome.json and tslint.json returns nothing, so no linter is configured at all; prettier appears only as a devDependency (package.json:232) with .prettierignore at the root.

**العلاج المقترح:** Either add `"lint": "prettier --check ."` to package.json and a `- name: Lint / run: pnpm lint` step before ci.yml:91 (adding an eslint config if the promise meant real linting), or record a ledger row saying the lint stage was deliberately replaced by the two tsc gates, and strike the claim from docs/DEPLOYMENT.md:7 so the pipeline stops being described as something it is not.

### 11-15-64 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:62`

**الادّعاء:** Two concurrent guest checkouts from the same phone collide on the phone unique index, and the shopper is told his EMAIL is taken

**الأثر المزعوم:** A guest shopper double-taps «تأكيد الطلب» on a slow Iraqi mobile connection — the exact behaviour the whole idempotency-key design exists for — and one of the two requests is refused with «هذا البريد مستخدم لحساب آخر في هذا المتجر — اتركه فارغاً أو سجّل الدخول.» He typed no email at all (registration is optional by design in ق-201), so the instruction is impossible to follow and names a field that is not on his screen. In a cash-on-delivery storefront there is no saved card and no receipt trail pulling him back: a refusal he cannot act on at the last step is an abandoned order. The identity step runs BEFORE the idempotency key can protect anything, so the key does not cover it.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:51 reads the shadow row — `prisma.customer.findFirst({ where: { storeId, phoneNormalized } })` — and :59 creates one when the read found nothing: `prisma.customer.create({ data: { storeId, fullName, phone, phoneNormalized, email, passwordHash: null, isGuest: true } })`. There is no transaction and no upsert between the two, so two requests that both read null both reach the create. The database then decides: prisma/migrations/20260818011500_storefront_customer_carts_reservations_9a/migration.sql:13 `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX customers_store_phone_present_uq ON customers(store_id, phone_normalized) WHERE phone_normalized IS NOT NULL` refuses the loser with P2002. The catch at :62-63 assumes only one index can ever fire — `// `customers_store_email_present_uq`: the email he typed is somebody's account here.` — and maps every P2002 to `409 EMAIL_TAKEN`. When the shopper left the email blank the email index at migration.sql:12 is partial on `email IS NOT NULL` and cannot fire at all, so the phone index is provably the one that threw and the message is provably wrong. Callers upstream cannot soften it: guestCheckout at :83-85 only reaches `priorSucceededCheckout` AFTER `guestCustomerFor` has returned.

**العلاج المقترح:** Replace the read-then-create at guest-checkout.service.ts:51-59 with an upsert on the phone key (`prisma.customer.upsert({ where: { storeId_phoneNormalized: { storeId, phoneNormalized } }, ... })`), keeping the account/disabled checks on the row it returns; failing that, inspect `error.meta.target` in the :62 handler and answer a phone collision by re-reading the row the winner just wrote and continuing, since that row is exactly the shadow customer this call was trying to obtain.

### 11-15-65 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:52`

**الادّعاء:** The unauthenticated guest-checkout door returns three distinguishable answers per phone number, disclosing whether a named person shops at a named store and whether that store has blocked him

**الأثر المزعوم:** One unauthenticated request classifies any Iraqi mobile number at any store into: not a customer / a customer / a customer the merchant has BLOCKED. For a pharmacy, a lingerie shop or a shop whose clientele is itself sensitive, that answers «does 0770XXXXXXX buy here?» for a stranger, one number at a time, at up to 40 numbers per IP per 10 minutes — roughly 5,700 numbers a day from a single address, and Iraqi mobile networks make a fresh address cheap. The 403 CUSTOMER_DISABLED branch is worse in kind: it publishes the merchant's own private moderation decision about a named person to anyone who asks. The platform elsewhere spends ~500 ms of bcrypt per request specifically to deny this exact fact.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1) phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:200-207 reaches guestCustomerFor with no session, needing only a phone and a two-character name. 2) services/guest-checkout.service.ts:51 looks the phone up; :52 answers 409 CUSTOMER_ACCOUNT_EXISTS «هذا الرقم مسجّل لدينا — سجّل الدخول لإتمام الطلب» for a registered account; :53 answers 403 CUSTOMER_DISABLED «تعذر إتمام الطلب بهذا الرقم — تواصل مع المتجر» for a customer the merchant deactivated (set by customer-directory.service.ts:134); anything else proceeds. Three codes, three facts. 3) The ceiling is http/shared.ts:118-132 via routes/storefront.ts:205 — 12 per caller-chosen key and 40 per IP per 10 minutes, which bounds bulk sweeps loosely and targeted lookups not at all. 4) The contradiction is explicit in the codebase: auth/auth.service.ts:289-298 runs an unconditional `verifyPassword(..., customer?.passwordHash ?? ABSENT_IDENTITY_HASH)` and auth/identity.ts:148-171 explains that the goal is to stop exactly this — «does this Iraqi mobile number shop at this named merchant?» — from being answered to a stranger.

**العلاج المقترح:** Make this door's answer independent of the row's state. Collapse guest-checkout.service.ts:52 and :53 into one generic refusal that is also what a caller sees for a phone with no row at all (e.g. always require sign-in once any row exists), so the response separates nothing; the honest, specific message belongs on the signed-in path, not on a public one.

### 11-15-66 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:83`

**الادّعاء:** Guest checkout declares and receives `cartVersion` but never compares it, so the optimistic-concurrency guard the signed-in path enforces does not exist for guests

**الأثر المزعوم:** A signed-in shopper whose cart changed between the summary he read and the tap on «تأكيد الطلب» is stopped with 409 CART_VERSION_CONFLICT and re-reads the total before committing. A guest is not: the client sends `cartVersion`, the server drops it and substitutes whatever the merge produced a millisecond earlier, so the order is created from the cart's current contents whatever the shopper was shown. He confirms a total of 45,000 and the courier arrives asking for a different amount — which in this market is a refused delivery at the door, the most expensive failure a COD shop has, and the one the version check exists to prevent. Guests are the majority of first-time buyers here, so the guard is missing precisely where it protects the most people. The function's own comment asserts the opposite, which is what will keep the gap from being noticed.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:71 declares `cartVersion: unknown` on `GuestCheckoutInput`, and :78-82 states «`cartVersion` is the MERGED cart's: the version the client sent guarded a cart the merge has just consumed, atomically, so the guard has been kept». It has not: :83-96 is the whole of `guestCheckout`, and `input.cartVersion` appears nowhere in it — :86 calls `mergeGuestCartIntoCustomer(storeId, customer.id, guestToken)`, whose signature (phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:534) takes no version and compares none, and :90 passes `cartVersion: cart.version`, a value the server just derived. The check it should feed is phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:833, `if (cart.version !== cartVersion) throw new HttpError(409, "CART_VERSION_CONFLICT", ...)`, which is therefore comparing a number against itself. The client does supply the real one: client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:196 sends `cartVersion` in the guest checkout body.

**العلاج المقترح:** Pass the client's `cartVersion` into `mergeGuestCartIntoCustomer` and assert it inside that serializable transaction against the guest cart's own `version` (which is what `currentCart` reports to a guest, so it is the number the client is holding) — throwing 409 CART_VERSION_CONFLICT when they differ, before any merge write, so the guest gets the same refusal-and-refresh the signed-in path gets.

### 11-15-67 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/merchant-store.service.ts:121`

**الادّعاء:** The `max_stores` plan check is an unlocked read-then-write, and its own comment claims a same-snapshot guarantee that READ COMMITTED does not give across transactions

**الأثر المزعوم:** Two store creations with different slugs in flight at once (two dashboard tabs, the switcher's inline form plus the Stores page form, or any script) both count the same N stores, both pass, and both insert — the merchant ends up above his plan's store ceiling. Because no surface can delete or deactivate a store, that over-cap state is permanent: it cannot be trimmed back by the merchant, by support, or by the platform owner. Nothing else breaks — later creations are still refused, existing stores keep working — so the cost is the plan boundary itself rather than a person's money, which is why this is ranked last.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1. phase1-backend/src/services/merchant-store.service.ts:119 opens `prisma.$transaction(...)` with no `isolationLevel`, and phase1-backend/src/db/prisma.ts:7 constructs `new PrismaClient()` with no default — so this runs at Postgres READ COMMITTED. 2. :121 `await assertWithinPlanLimit(transaction, merchantId, "max_stores")` → entitlement.service.ts:110-121, which reads the limit (:111) and then the usage (:113) via merchantUsage → entitlement.service.ts:69 `client.store.count({ where: { merchantId } })`, and only compares (:114). 3. :126 `createStoreFoundation(..., transaction)` → store-bootstrap.service.ts:28 `client.store.create(...)`. Between the count and the insert there is no row lock, no advisory lock, and no database constraint on stores-per-merchant (prisma/schema.prisma's Store model has uniques on slug, custom_domain and default_subdomain only), so a concurrent transaction's count cannot see the other's uncommitted row. 4. entitlement.service.ts:107-108 asserts the opposite: «Runs inside the caller's transaction when one is provided so the check and the write see the same snapshot» — true within one transaction, false as protection against a second writer. 5. The codebase applies the correct pattern elsewhere and says why: entitlement.service.ts:216-218, «The partial unique index `plans_single_recommended` backs the same invariant at the database layer, so two racing owner sessions cannot both win». phase1-backend/src/db/serializable-retry.ts:64 `withSerializableRetry` exists for exactly this and is not used on this path.

**العلاج المقترح:** Serialize per merchant at the top of the transaction, before the count: `await transaction.$executeRaw`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended(${merchantId}::text, 0))`` (or a `SELECT ... FROM merchants WHERE id = ... FOR UPDATE`), so two creations for the same merchant queue behind each other and the second one counts the first. Correct the comment at entitlement.service.ts:107-108 to say what the transaction actually guarantees, since three other gated writes read it as protection.

### 11-15-68 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/soft-delete.service.ts:264`

**الادّعاء:** The LAST_TEAM_MANAGER invariant is enforced only on the removal path, with an unlocked SELECT inside a READ COMMITTED transaction, and not at all on the three other paths that reach the same end state

**الأثر المزعوم:** A store can be left with no staff member who can manage its own roster, and the guard that exists to prevent it can be walked past three ways without any race at all: PATCH the other manager to isActive:false, PATCH him down to store_staff, or PUT a `team.manage` deny override on him — none of those consult the invariant — after which the DELETE guard skips itself because it only runs on an active membership. Under two concurrent removals it fails even on its own path: each transaction's survivor query sees the other manager still live and both commit. The cost stays low only because the owning merchant holds every permission code unconditionally and can always rebuild the team; a merchant who has delegated the store to two managers and does not have the dashboard open will still hit a dead end and a support call.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/soft-delete.service.ts:253 opens `prisma.$transaction(...)` with no isolationLevel — the Postgres default, READ COMMITTED (contrast phase1-backend/src/services/pos.service.ts:431, which passes `Prisma.TransactionIsolationLevel.Serializable` explicitly for exactly this class of check). The guard at :263 runs only `if (membership.isActive && resolvePermissionSet(...).has("team.manage"))`, and its survivor query at :264-267 is a plain `findMany` — no `FOR UPDATE`, no lock — even though this same file already has the locking idiom it needs at :337-343 (`lockTrashedRow`, whose docstring at :324-336 explains precisely why a plain findFirst cannot serialize two writers). Two removals therefore each see the other as `stillManaged` at :268 and both reach the update at :291. The unguarded paths to the same state are phase1-backend/src/services/staff.service.ts:121-152 (updateStoreMembership accepts `isActive:false` and `roleCode:"store_staff"` with no last-manager check anywhere in the function) and :154-182 (setMembershipOverrides accepts `[{permissionCode:"team.manage", effect:"deny"}]`, which resolvePermissionSet at phase1-backend/src/auth/staff-permissions.ts:70 removes from a store_manager's set). The safety net that keeps this low is phase1-backend/src/auth/staff-permissions.ts:247, where the merchant branch returns every code.

**العلاج المقترح:** Replace the survivor `findMany` at phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/soft-delete.service.ts:264-267 with a `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` over the store's live memberships, in the style of lockTrashedRow at :337-343, so two removals serialize. Then lift the check into a shared helper and call it from updateStoreMembership (before an `isActive:false` or a demotion) and from setMembershipOverrides (before a `team.manage` deny), and drop the `membership.isActive` precondition at :263 so suspending first no longer disarms it.

### 11-15-69 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/staff.service.ts:155`

**الادّعاء:** `setMembershipOverrides` is the one team mutation that skips `assertDemoWritable`, so any visitor to the public demo can strip the seeded demo staff account of every permission until the nightly reseed

**الأثر المزعوم:** In the demo environment `POST /public/demo-login` hands any anonymous visitor a merchant session on the demo store, and that merchant holds `team.manage`. Its two sibling mutations both refuse to touch the seeded `staff.demo@codiq.iq` membership — `updateStoreMembership` because disabling it «would kill the demo staff login documented for prospects until the nightly reseed», and `softDeleteMembership` for the same reason. `PUT …/team/:membershipId/overrides` does not, so one request with `[{permissionCode:"orders.view",effect:"deny"}, {"catalog.products.view","deny"}, {"inventory.update","deny"}, {"pos.sell","deny"}]` leaves the documented staff walkthrough showing an empty sidebar for every prospect who arrives afterwards, until the 01:00 TRUNCATE. It is the same demo-integrity contract the module states as its third guarantee, defeated through the one call site that forgot to invoke it.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1) `setMembershipOverrides` calls `requireMembershipInStore` and discards the row, with no demo guard anywhere in the function: phase1-backend/src/services/staff.service.ts:154-182 (the guard is absent at :155). 2) Its two siblings do call it: staff.service.ts:126 (`assertDemoWritable("demo_staff", current.staff.email)`) with the reasoning at :123-125, and phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/soft-delete.service.ts:259. 3) The email needed for the check is already loaded — `requireMembershipInStore` selects `staff: { select: { email: true } }` at staff.service.ts:116 — so the guard costs no extra query, exactly as demo-mode.ts:127 intends. 4) The route is reachable in the demo: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/team.ts:33-38, and `/merchant/...` is not among `DEMO_BLOCKED_PREFIXES` (phase1-backend/src/demo/demo-mode.ts:50). 5) The session is public: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/public.ts:101-111 registers `POST /public/demo-login` when `config.demoMode` and issues the demo MERCHANT session. 6) The contract broken: phase1-backend/src/demo/demo-mode.ts:16-17 and the `demo_staff` message at :119.

**العلاج المقترح:** Bind the row `requireMembershipInStore` already returns and call the guard, one line: `const current = await requireMembershipInStore(storeId, membershipId); assertDemoWritable("demo_staff", current.staff.email);` at phase1-backend/src/services/staff.service.ts:155, matching updateStoreMembership at :122-126.

### 11-15-70 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/tests/csrf-origin.guard.mjs:50`

**الادّعاء:** csrf-origin.guard.mjs computes each route's inspection window with `indexOf("\n  app.")`, hard-coding two-space indentation, and the codebase already contains a write route at four spaces

**الأثر المزعوم:** The guard is the platform's second CSRF layer and the memory that keeps it applied — its own header says 21 of 109 write routes had drifted off the convention and that this file is what stops the twenty-second. Its window over-runs any registration that is not indented by exactly two spaces, so a write route can pass by borrowing a neighbour's `requireTrustedWriteOrigin` call. This is latent rather than live today, but the one place it can fire is already built: `if (config.demoMode) { … }` in public.ts, whose contents sit at four spaces. Add a second demo-only write route *before* the existing one and it ships with no origin check and a green `pnpm csrf:guard`. In the demo environment that route would be reachable cross-site with `codiq_session` attached, and `POST /public/demo-login` (public.ts:102) hands out a merchant session to anyone who asks.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1. `phase1-backend/tests/csrf-origin.guard.mjs:36` — `const WRITE = /app\.(post|put|patch|delete)\(\s*"([^"]+)"/g;` matches a registration at ANY indentation. 2. `phase1-backend/tests/csrf-origin.guard.mjs:47-51` — the window is `source.slice(start, source.indexOf("\n  app.", start + 1))`, on the stated assumption that «Routes are written one per statement here (single-line or block)». The literal `"\n  app."` also assumes exactly two spaces, which is the part the comment does not say and the code depends on. 3. `phase1-backend/tests/csrf-origin.guard.mjs:52` — `if (body.includes("requireTrustedWriteOrigin")) continue;` — a substring test over that window, so anything the window swallows counts as this route's guard. 4. `phase1-backend/src/http/routes/public.ts:101-111` — `if (config.demoMode) {` opens a block whose `app.post("/public/demo-login", …)` at :102 is indented four spaces. Scanning all 37 files in `phase1-backend/src/http/routes/`, this is the only write registration not at two spaces, so it is the one and only window that currently over-runs: it starts at :102 and does not close until the next two-space registration, `app.get("/public/unsubscribe/:token"` at :125. 5. Today that window still contains this route's own `requireTrustedWriteOrigin(request)` at :103, so the guard passes for the right reason. Reverse the order — an unguarded write route at :102 and the guarded one after it — and the unguarded one's window reaches the guarded one's call and the guard reports `passed`.

**العلاج المقترح:** Stop inferring the window from indentation. Either anchor the scan on the next match of the same `app.<method>(` / `app.get(` pattern regardless of leading whitespace (`/\n\s*app\.(get|post|put|patch|delete|all|use)\(/`), or, better, require the check to appear in the FIRST statement of the handler rather than anywhere in a text window — the convention the guard's own error message already states («أضف requireTrustedWriteOrigin(request) كأول سطر»). Prove the change by reintroducing the defect: move a second, unguarded `app.post` above `/public/demo-login` inside the demo block and confirm `pnpm csrf:guard` fails.

## المراحل 16 → 20

**81 نتيجة** رفعها الوكلاء · **26** منها فقط بلغت التدقيق المعاكس (**12** سقطت · **14** صمدت). الباقي **لم يُدقَّق بعد** — وهذا هو الحدّ الذي يجب أن يُقرأ به هذا الملفّ كلّه.

### 16-20-01 · **عالية** · `client/src/components/CouponsManager.tsx:61`

**الادّعاء:** A coupon whose «ينتهي في» is picked from the date box expires at 03:00 Baghdad on that day, so the advertised last day of every campaign is refused — and the merchant's own list tells him the day is included.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant announces «الكوبون حتى ٣٠/٩» and every shopper who types it on the 30th after 3 AM is told «الكوبون خارج فترة صلاحيته.» — the busiest day of the campaign is lost, the shop is blamed, and the merchant cannot find the fault because his coupon list prints «ينتهي ٣٠/٩». The same arithmetic makes «يبدأ في ١ رمضان» dead for the first three hours of the launch night, which in Iraq is prime shopping time.

**الدليل المزعوم:** CouponsManager.tsx:176-177 — the two `<input type="date">` fields put "YYYY-MM-DD" into draft.startsAt/endsAt. CouponsManager.tsx:60-61 — draftBody sends `new Date(draft.endsAt).toISOString()`; a date-only ISO string is parsed as UTC midnight, so "2026-09-30" becomes "2026-09-30T00:00:00.000Z". phase1-backend/src/http/schemas/marketing.schema.ts:50-55 — optionalCouponDate only checks the string parses; no end-of-day rule. phase1-backend/src/services/coupon.service.ts:180-186 — optionalDate stores `new Date(value)` verbatim (create :242-243, update :292-293). coupon.service.ts:52-56 — assertCouponWindowAndActive throws COUPON_EXPIRED when `coupon.endsAt < now`; it runs under the checkout lock at :101 (lockCouponForCheckout, `new Date()`) and in the preview at :142. Baghdad is a fixed +03:00 (store-traffic.service.ts:16), so 00:00Z is 03:00 on the chosen day. Shopper copy: client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:353 «الكوبون خارج فترة صلاحيته.». The merchant cannot see it: CouponsManager.tsx:191 renders `new Date(coupon.endsAt).toLocaleDateString("ar-IQ")`, and 03:00 local on the 30th prints as the 30th; :148-149 `.slice(0, 10)` round-trips the same day back into the editor, so re-saving changes nothing. The suite never posts a date-only value: phase1-backend/tests/phase20b1.integration.mjs:194 uses full timestamps.

**العلاج المقترح:** Give a bare `YYYY-MM-DD` Baghdad day semantics at ONE place — coupon.service.ts optionalDate: if the value matches /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/, store startsAt as `${value}T00:00:00+03:00` and endsAt as `${value}T23:59:59.999+03:00` (the field name decides which). Then CouponsManager.draftBody can send draft.startsAt/draft.endsAt as typed (drop the `new Date(...).toISOString()`), and the list keeps printing the intended day.

### 16-20-02 · **عالية** · `client/src/components/ProductCard.tsx:42`

**الادّعاء:** The one shared product card adds a multi-variant product to the cart by bare productId, so the home rails, catalog grid and category page silently add the implicit default variant — or answer «not found» once the merchant disables that placeholder.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A shopper on the home rail sees «قميص 12,000–15,000 د.ع», taps «أضف إلى السلة», and receives the product-named default variant at its price without ever being asked for a size or colour. In cash-on-delivery the wrong size is discovered at the door; the refused delivery is the normal Iraqi return, so the merchant pays the courier both ways and restocks through RI-1. When the merchant did the natural thing for a sized product and disabled the placeholder default (the UI offers «تعطيل» on it), every card tap on a product the card itself marks available answers RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND. The 20A clause «عرض المتغيرات واختيارها في الـStorefront» is kept only on the product page.

**الدليل المزعوم:** publicProducts (storefront.service.ts:365-381) returns minPriceIqd/maxPriceIqd (:373-374) and isAvailable = ANY active variant with stock (:380) — no variant count or ids; the client type agrees (storefront-api.ts:62-71). ProductCard.tsx:2-4 (ق-203) is the ONE card for home rails, catalog grid and category page; :20-21 prints the range when prices differ; :42-43 enables the button on isAvailable and calls onAdd(product.id). StorefrontCatalogPage.tsx:77 and StorefrontHomePage.tsx:118 call storefrontApi.addCartItem(slug, productId, 1) with no variantId. Server addCartItem (storefront.service.ts:456-465): with no variantId it resolves { productId, isDefault: true, isActive: true } (:463) and adds that row, or throws 404 RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND when the default is inactive (:465). The merchant can disable the default: VariantManager.tsx:152/179 toggles isActive on every row incl. «افتراضي», and updateVariant (product-catalog.service.ts:490-493) has no isDefault guard — only delete is protected (:524). The product page does it right (StorefrontProductPage.tsx:82-84 picker when >1 variant, :93 sends selectedVariant.id) and the 20A browser suite exercises only that path (phase20a.browser-acceptance.mjs:157-169); the card path is never tested.

**العلاج المقترح:** Return an active variantCount from publicProducts (a _count on the existing variants include) and, in ProductCard, when variantCount > 1 render the button as a Link to the product page labelled «اختر الخيارات» instead of calling onAdd. Server-side, in addCartItem refuse a bare productId when the product has more than one active variant with 422 VARIANT_REQUIRED, so no client can add an unchosen variant.

### 16-20-03 · **عالية** · `client/src/components/ProductCatalogManager.tsx:114`

**الادّعاء:** The merchant's products page and the «الجرد» screen fetch only the newest 100 products and never read nextCursor, so a growth-plan store's older products disappear from editing and from low-stock alerts while the screen reports a false count

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant on the growth plan (1,000 products — prisma/migrations/20260825150000_phase16_plans_entitlements/migration.sql:73) with 300 products reads «100 منتج في المتجر الحالي» (line 221), a false number, and cannot edit, hide, remove or stock-adjust products 101–300 except by typing each name into search. Worse: the «الجرد» screen computes its low-stock list from that same 100-row array (147, 220), so the 200 older staples — the ones that run low every week — never appear, and the screen says «لا توجد تنبيهات نفاد أو انخفاض مخزون حاليًا» while they are out of stock. Silent, daily, and it is the sales loss the low-stock screen exists to prevent. Free-plan merchants are spared only because their cap is exactly 100 (migration.sql:68).

**الدليل المزعوم:** client/src/components/ProductCatalogManager.tsx:114 fetches `${base}/products?limit=100${query}` once and reads only `products`; the file contains no read of `nextCursor` and no «تحميل المزيد» (grep of the file: zero matches). Server side: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/catalog.ts:65 calls `listProducts(store.id, limit, cursor, lowStock === "true", q)`; phase1-backend/src/services/product-catalog.service.ts:237 runs `parseListLimit`, which rejects anything above 100 (phase1-backend/src/services/list-page.ts:54 LIST_LIMIT_MAX = 100, :255-256 throws INVALID_LIMIT) so the client cannot ask for more; :266-268 reads `take: limit + 1` and returns `{ products, nextCursor }`. Back in the client: :147 `lowStock = products.filter(...)` over the 100-row array; :220 renders `lowStock.map(...)` and the «لا توجد تنبيهات» empty state from it; :221 prints `${products.length} منتج في المتجر الحالي`. The server's own `lowStock=true` filter (catalog.ts:65) is never sent — :114 builds only `limit` and `q`. TillPage.tsx:314 already pages the same endpoint by cursor, so the pattern exists in the codebase.

**العلاج المقترح:** In `refresh`, follow `nextCursor` until null (as client/src/pages/TillPage.tsx:314 does for this same endpoint) or add a «تحميل المزيد» button bound to `nextCursor` as OrderWorkspace.tsx:382 does; in «الجرد» mode request `?lowStock=true` (paged the same way) instead of filtering the first page in the browser; print the total only when `nextCursor` is null, otherwise «أحدث 100 منتج».

### 16-20-04 · **عالية** · `client/src/pages/PasswordResetRequest.tsx:108`

**الادّعاء:** A shopper who registered by phone only and forgets his password has no recovery path at all — the screen sends him to a merchant who has no tool, re-registering is refused in English, and guest checkout on his own number is refused too

**الأثر المزعوم:** The ordinary Iraqi shopper: reaches the shop by phone, leaves the optional email blank, and gets an 8-hour session so he re-types the password almost every visit. The day he forgets it he cannot sign in, cannot reset (email-only), cannot re-register on his number, and cannot even order as a guest on that number (CUSTOMER_ACCOUNT_EXISTS fires before guest checkout). He is told «تواصل مع المتجر لاستعادة حسابك» but the merchant has no button — the only real path is a manual DB update by an engineer, which the PR-1 header itself called launch-blocking for merchants. Net effect: a lost sale per forgotten password, and the merchant is blamed on the phone for a platform gap.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Registration email optional: client/src/pages/CustomerAuthPage.tsx:78 «البريد الإلكتروني (اختياري)» → phase1-backend/src/auth/auth.service.ts:255 normalizeOptionalEmail. Login is phone+password: auth.service.ts:286-300; cookie/session 8h: phase1-backend/src/http/shared.ts:72 and phase1-backend/src/config.ts:60. Recovery is email-only: phase1-backend/src/http/schemas/password-reset.schema.ts:23-25 → phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:148-152 → phase1-backend/src/services/password-reset.service.ts:251 (findFirst by email). Screen fallback: PasswordResetRequest.tsx:108. Merchant has no tool: phase1-backend/src/services/customer-directory.service.ts:124 (only setCustomerActive; no password function) and phase1-backend/src/http/routes/account-security.ts:8-10 (setting another's password 'belongs with the account-lifecycle work' — not built). Re-register path: auth.service.ts:260 looks up isGuest:true only → :268 create → unique violation → phase1-backend/src/http/errors.ts:206 'This phone is already registered in this store.' (English) → client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:335-373 has no DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_PHONE entry so :373 prints the English message. Guest checkout refused on a registered phone: phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:52 (after the allowed check at :44). ق-125 (docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:222) defers WhatsApp OTP to 25ب but says nothing about recovery for phone-only accounts.

**العلاج المقترح:** Add a merchant-side recovery action behind settings.manage in customer-directory.service.ts: `resetCustomerPassword(storeId, customerId)` that mints a random temporary password with createPasswordHash, calls revokeAllSessionsWithin(tx,'customer',id,now) and invalidateLiveResetTokens in one transaction, writes an audit event, and returns the temporary value once for the merchant to pass by phone/WhatsApp; surface it as «إعادة تعيين كلمة سر الزبون» in the customer directory. Then change PasswordResetRequest.tsx:108 to say that the shop can reset it from its dashboard, add DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_PHONE → «هذا الرقم مسجّل في هذا المتجر — سجّل الدخول أو استعد كلمة السر» to storefront-api.ts messages, and consider a longer customer session (e.g. 30 days) so the password is asked for rarely.

### 16-20-05 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/auth/auth.service.ts:260`

**الادّعاء:** Phone verification/OTP never shipped, so registering with someone else's number claims that shopper's guest row — his orders and addresses — at any store where he bought as a guest

**الأثر المزعوم:** Any Iraqi shopper who bought cash-on-delivery without an account, or whom a merchant hand-entered from his notebook, can be impersonated by anyone who knows his mobile number: the impersonator types that number and any password on the public register form and is signed in as him — order history, delivery addresses, the ability to cancel his open orders, request returns on his delivered ones and place COD orders under his name. This is the exact harm the debt register predicted for D-12 («انتحال هوية زبون برقم هاتف الغير») and named the 19-أ OTP as the fix. The per-IP ceiling of 8 does not stop a targeted claim (one request) but does hit a neighbourhood sharing one mobile IP.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Promise: task brief 19-أ «email and phone verification and OTP for customer sign-in»; docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:38 (D-12 «تنفيذ المرحلة 19 كاملة + إضافة صريحة: OTP دخول الزبون عبر SMS/واتساب») and :142 (→ 19-أ). Code: no OTP/verification exists — every `otp` hit in src is TOTP or the REAUTH constants; `customer` has no verified-at column read anywhere. Guest rows are minted on a bare phone by guest checkout (phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:51-59, `isGuest: true`, `passwordHash: null`) and by the merchant's hand-add (phase1-backend/src/services/manual-provisioning.service.ts:134-140). The public register route (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:114-125) rate-limits 8/IP (:117), parses name+phone+password (:118) and calls `registerCustomer` (:119). `registerCustomer` normalises the phone (auth.service.ts:254), looks up `{storeId, phoneNormalized, isGuest: true}` (:260) and, if found, `customer.update({ ...data, isGuest: false })` on THAT row (:267) — no possession proof of the number; the comment at :257-259 says «gets HIS row — orders, addresses and points included». The route then mints a customer session for `account.id` (storefront.ts:120-121), which reaches `/account/orders` (:221-223), addresses (:164-165), self-cancel (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/customer-delight.ts:77) and returns (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/returns.ts:34). Ledger ق-201 (docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:300) records the claim rule «التسجيل لاحقاً بالهاتف نفسه يستملك الصفّ بطلباته (D-99)» with no verification step.

**العلاج المقترح:** Gate the ق-201 claim on possession of the number: when `registerCustomer` finds a guest row, send a one-time code to that phone (WhatsApp/SMS — the 19-أ channel) and only run the `isGuest:false` update after the code is presented. Until a channel exists, refuse to claim a guest row that already has orders or addresses with a 409 directing the shopper to the store (the same shape `guestCustomerFor` uses at guest-checkout.service.ts:52), or create a fresh row and let the merchant merge.

### 16-20-06 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/auth/auth.service.ts:122`

**الادّعاء:** The owner's second factor is opt-in and switchable off with a password — the code is the «ready to enable later» state the phase promised not to ship, and the acceptance test asserts the weaker rule

**الأثر المزعوم:** The one account that owns every merchant's data is protected by a single 8-character password until the owner chooses to enrol — and ledger ق-129 lowered the floor to 8 on the stated premise that «حساب المالك محميّ بما هو أقوى من الطول: TOTP». ق-150 records that in production the factor was impossible to enable for a whole month; by construction it is still off for any owner who has not pressed the card. The acceptance criterion «owner login without the second factor refused» holds only after voluntary enrolment, and a session holder can remove the factor with the password alone, which is what makes finding 2 reachable.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Promise: task brief 19-أ «2FA actually ON for the owner (not "ready to enable later")»; docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:95 (ق-48 ب «2FA مفعّلاً لحساب المالك»), :220 (ق-123 «TOTP يحقق شرط ق-48 «مفعّل فعلياً لا بنية جاهزة»»), :221 (ق-124 «العامل الثاني إلزامي على كل من يحمل رمز صلاحية منصة، لا على المالك وحده … الإلزام عمود في القاعدة لا إعداد في الواجهة»), :226 (ق-129 floor lowered citing TOTP). Code: `loginPlatformOwner` checks the password (auth.service.ts:98-101) then `if (owner.totpEnabledAt)` (:102) — an unenrolled owner falls through to :122 and receives a full session on the password alone. `requirePlatformOwner` (phase1-backend/src/http/shared.ts:206-213) verifies the session and returns — no enrolment gate — whereas `requirePlatformStaff` refuses an unenrolled staff row with SECOND_FACTOR_ENROLMENT_REQUIRED (phase1-backend/src/auth/platform-permissions.ts:124-126) and the staff shell blocks on it (client/src/pages/PlatformStaffDashboard.tsx:225-226); nothing equivalent exists for the owner (client grep: OwnerAccountPanels.tsx:172 only begins enrolment on click). `disableTotp` (phase1-backend/src/services/owner-totp.service.ts:134-156) clears secret and enabledAt on the current password only. Test: phase1-backend/tests/phase19b.owner-totp.integration.mjs:102 asserts «he can still sign in with the password alone» before enrolment and :210 «the password alone signs him in again» after disable; the refusal at :123-126 is asserted only for an enrolled owner.

**العلاج المقترح:** Mirror the staff rule: in `requirePlatformOwner` refuse `totpEnabledAt === null` with SECOND_FACTOR_ENROLMENT_REQUIRED except on the enrolment routes (an `allowUnenrolled` option like platform-permissions.ts:103-106), and block the owner shell on that code the way PlatformStaffDashboard.tsx:225 does; make `disableTotp` require a fresh TOTP/recovery code and redirect straight into re-enrolment rather than leaving the factor off; flip the test assertions at :102 and :210 to expect 403/refusal.

### 16-20-07 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/http/routes/orders.ts:158`

**الادّعاء:** A pharmacy order sits in «بانتظار رفع الوصفة» with no way for the shop to attach the prescription it already received on WhatsApp — the only merchant exits are cancel or reject.

**الأثر المزعوم:** The normal Iraqi pharmacy customer phones or WhatsApps the prescription photo; the pharmacist is holding it and the order is stuck in `new` because «قيد المعالجة» is disabled until an upload that only the customer's browser session can perform. The shop's choices are to cancel a legitimate order or to fulfil it off-platform. A guest shopper (phone, no password) who did not upload in the same browser tab can never come back to do it, because there is no login without a password.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Checkout stamps the gate: phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:992-993 `prescriptionStatus: requiresPrescription ? "pending_upload" : null`. The gate: phase1-backend/src/services/order-lifecycle.service.ts:253-254 refuses new→processing unless approved; :15 the only other edges from `new` are cancelled/rejected. The only upload route is customer-session-only: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:227-231 (`requireStorefrontCustomer`, `uploadOrderPrescription(store.id, customer.id, …)`), and prescription.service.ts:28-33 ownedOrder requires the customerId to match. Merchant/staff have GET (orders.ts:152-156) and review (orders.ts:158-162) only; reviewOrderPrescription (prescription.service.ts:121-123) refuses anything but pending_review — no merchant write can ever move pending_upload forward. Merchant screen offers no action: client/src/components/OrderWorkspace.tsx:643 «قيد المعالجة» معطّل: بانتظار رفع الزبون للوصفة الطبية. … الإلغاء أو الرفض متاحان دون اعتماد.» and :655 «لم يرفع الزبون الوصفة بعد؛ يظهر الملف هنا فور رفعه.». Guest path: storefront.ts:207-209 issues a session cookie at guest checkout, but the row has passwordHash null (guest-checkout.service.ts:59) and loginCustomer needs a password (auth.service.ts:289), so once that cookie is gone (other phone, cleared browser, expiry per identity.ts:198) the shopper cannot reach the page the product told him to use (StorefrontProductPage.tsx:144 «…سيُطلب منك رفع صورة الوصفة من صفحة الطلب…»).

**العلاج المقترح:** Add `POST /merchant/stores/:storeSlug/orders/:orderId/prescription` behind requireStoreActor("orders.update") that reuses putPrescriptionImage + the guarded updateMany from uploadOrderPrescription (pending_upload|rejected → pending_review), storing the actor kind/id on the order_prescriptions row so the audit shows the shop attached it; charge the merchant's max_image_storage_mb the same way. In OrderWorkspace.tsx, replace the «لم يرفع الزبون الوصفة بعد» dead end with an «رفع الوصفة نيابةً عن الزبون» file control when the status is pending_upload or rejected.

### 16-20-08 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/notifications/outbox-worker.ts:164`

**الادّعاء:** The trial-ending email promises «لا يتوقف بيع», but landing on the free plan closes the storefront checkout at once for any merchant already past 200 orders this month — and nothing tells him it happened

**الأثر المزعوم:** The merchant this hits is the successful one — the trial merchant whose month went well, or the free-plan merchant who just crossed his 201st order. From that moment every shopper at his checkout is refused with «المتجر لا يستقبل طلبات جديدة حالياً — تواصل مع المتجر» until the 1st of the next UTC month; the code's own comment says a refused COD shopper «closes the tab». The merchant's phone simply stops ringing. No screen he lands on and no message he receives says why; the only place the truth is written is a usage bar on a panel he has to think to open. The reminder he was sent three days earlier told him, verbatim, that selling would not stop.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1. Free plan max_monthly_orders = 200: prisma/migrations/20260825150000_phase16_plans_entitlements/migration.sql:70. 2. Usage is every non-POS order across the merchant's stores since the 1st of the UTC month, regardless of plan at the time: phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:62-64 (monthStartUtc) and :79 (storeOrder.count createdAt >= monthStart, source != pos). 3. Trial lapses → scanner writes a free-plan row: phase1-backend/src/notifications/trial-expiry-scanner.ts:70 (finds endsAt <= now) → :96-98 (creates free row). From this instant the resolver returns free's limits: entitlement.service.ts:52-59. 4. Checkout gate: phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:861 assertWithinPlanLimit(..., "max_monthly_orders") → entitlement.service.ts:114 (usage + 1 > 200 → throw) → storefront.service.ts:863-865 rethrown as 409 STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_ORDERS «المتجر لا يستقبل طلبات جديدة حالياً — تواصل مع المتجر». Shopper-side rendering: client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:366. The code's own reading of what a refused COD shopper does: storefront.service.ts:913-918 («he closes the tab»). 5. What the merchant was told three days earlier, verbatim: phase1-backend/src/notifications/outbox-worker.ts:164 «متجرك يبقى مفتوحاً، وطلباتك وزبائنك ومنتجاتك كما هي — لا يُحذف شيء ولا يتوقف بيع.» — false for any merchant whose month-to-date non-POS orders ≥ 200 at the moment step 3 runs. The ledger records the same promise as intent: docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:286 (ق-186 «النصّ العربيّ يقول صراحةً إنّ المتجر يبقى مفتوحاً»). 6. No merchant-facing signal exists when the cap fires: the merchant home service never reads entitlements (grep for entitlement|subscription|max_monthly_orders|PLAN_LIMIT in phase1-backend/src/services/merchant-home.service.ts → no matches); no notification event kind for a plan limit exists (grep plan.limit|limit_reached|monthly_orders in phase1-backend/src/notifications → no matches; the only subscription kind is trial.ending, prisma/migrations/20260909110000_q186_trial_ending_reminder/migration.sql:29-30); the landing-page probe reads only plan.code (client/src/pages/Home.tsx:661-665). The sole surface is client/src/components/SubscriptionPanel.tsx:118-131, a usage bar shown only when he opens that panel. 7. Ledger ق-216 (docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:315) justified moving the message to the shopper on the grounds that «شاشات التاجر تبقى على PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED» — true only for the merchant's own writes (e.g. POST /merchant/stores, phase16.plans-entitlements.integration.ts:166-168), never for the shoppers' refusals, which are the writes that matter here. ADR-005 line 33-34 decided hard enforcement in checkout; it did not decide silence.

**العلاج المقترح:** Two small changes. (a) Make the sentence true: in outbox-worker.ts:164-165 replace «لا يُحذف شيء ولا يتوقف بيع» with the real rule, and have remindEndingTrials (trial-expiry-scanner.ts:146-163) include the merchant's month-to-date order count against the free plan's max_monthly_orders in the payload so the email can say plainly when he is already above it. (b) Tell him when it fires: in the checkout route's error path (outside the rolled-back transaction), on STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_ORDERS enqueue one operational notification_outbox row per merchant per UTC month (dedupeKey `plan_orders_cap:<merchantId>:<yyyy-mm>`, storeId null like trial.ending, event kind added by migration), and add an `ordersCapReached` flag to the merchant-home payload so the dashboard shows a banner with the PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED sentence and the upgrade button.

### 16-20-09 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/coupon.service.ts:73`

**الادّعاء:** The coupon «حد الاستخدام لكل زبون» is unenforceable for ق-201 guest checkouts: the per-customer count is keyed on a customer row minted from any unverified phone the shopper types, while the courier's phone is a separate field.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant who runs «خصم 40٪ لأول طلب — مرة واحدة لكل زبون» ships that discount to the same household as many times as someone re-types one digit of the identity phone; the delivery address and the courier's number stay identical every time. Because the model is cash on delivery, the goods leave before anyone can notice, and the only ceiling left is 40 guest checkouts per 10 minutes per store+IP — an IP a whole neighbourhood shares, so it cannot be tightened without refusing real shoppers. The 20B clause «حدود استخدام» is met for accounts and void for the population ق-201 was built for.

**الدليل المزعوم:** CouponsManager.tsx:175 offers «حد الاستخدام لكل زبون (اختياري)» → stored as maxUsesPerCustomer (coupon.service.ts:258). At checkout storefront.service.ts:881 calls lockCouponForCheckout(tx, storeId, customerId, code) → coupon.service.ts:112 → assertCouponUsageWithinLimits, whose per-customer branch (coupon.service.ts:72-75) counts couponRedemption rows for THAT customerId only. Guest path: routes/storefront.ts:206-210 → guestCustomerFor (guest-checkout.service.ts:43-67) keys the customer row on normalizePhone(input.phone) (:47, :51) and CREATES a fresh row for any phone not seen before (:59) — no verification exists (ledger ق-125, DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:222: OTP waits for 25ب). guestCheckout then calls checkout(storeId, customer.id) (guest-checkout.service.ts:88), so a fresh row always has 0 redemptions. The phone the courier calls is a different value: address.phoneContact → storefront.service.ts:624 phoneContactNormalized → order.recipientPhone (:993); the client only pre-fills it while empty (StorefrontCheckoutPage.tsx:151), so identity phone and delivery phone are independent inputs. The sole ceiling is routes/storefront.ts:205 limitStorefrontWriteWithAddressCeiling(…, 12, 40) → shared.ts:126-131 = 40 per 10 min per store+IP. Nothing in coupon.service.ts or CouponsManager.tsx mentions guests; previewCoupon (coupon.service.ts:143) reports 0 uses for the fresh row too.

**العلاج المقترح:** Count per-customer redemptions by the delivery phone as well as the customer id: storefront.service.ts:868 already has address.phoneContactNormalized before the coupon lock at :881 — pass it into lockCouponForCheckout and, in assertCouponUsageWithinLimits, count couponRedemption where { customerId } OR { order: { recipientPhone: phone } } (recipientPhone is on the order row, storefront.service.ts:993). Optionally add a coupon flag «للحسابات المسجلة فقط» that refuses customer.isGuest rows with a named 422, and say in CouponsManager that «لكل زبون» today means «لكل رقم هاتف».

### 16-20-10 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:79`

**الادّعاء:** Cancelled, rejected and refused-at-the-door orders spend the monthly order quota, so a burst of junk COD orders — or an ordinary month of refusals — closes an honest shop's storefront until the 1st

**الأثر المزعوم:** The free plan allows 200 orders a month. The counter is every non-POS StoreOrder row created this month regardless of status, so the merchant's own act of rejecting a fake order, or the shopper cancelling, or the courier being turned away at the door (which the ledger itself calls the majority of Iraqi COD returns) never gives the quota back. The per-address checkout wall is deliberately loose at 40 per 10 minutes so a neighbourhood behind one mobile IP is never blocked — which means one phone with a script exhausts a free shop's entire month in well under an hour, and the storefront then answers every honest shopper «المتجر لا يستقبل طلبات جديدة حالياً» until the calendar month ends. The merchant sees «طلبات الشهر 200 / 200» with no explanation that the 60 orders he rejected are inside that number, and his only exit is a manual upgrade request to a paid plan approved by hand by the owner. The quota was designed (ق-135/ق-136) to measure platform-carried volume; a rejected order carried no money.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:79 — client.storeOrder.count({ where: { store: { merchantId }, createdAt: { gte: monthStartUtc() }, source: { not: "pos" } } }) — no status predicate; the only exclusion is POS (ق-135). entitlement.service.ts:110-114 — usage + 1 > limit refuses. phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:861-864 — every storefront checkout runs this check and is refused 409 STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_ORDERS at the ceiling. phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:170-183,203-205 — checkout limiter is 12 per shopper and 40 per address, and guest checkout is keyed on a caller-chosen cart token or the IP; phase1-backend/src/http/shared.ts:126-131 — the address window is 10 minutes, so 40 orders / 10 min per IP. storefront.ts:176-178 in the repo's own words: «A shop drowning in fake COD orders cannot tell which ones to phone». docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:237 (ق-140): «كل توصيلٍ مرفوض (أغلب مرتجعات COD العراقية)». docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:233 (ق-136): the quota exists so a busy till does not «تحجب زبائن المتجر الإلكتروني أنفسهم عند الدفع» — the same harm now arrives through junk rows. prisma/migrations/20260825150000_phase16_plans_entitlements/migration.sql:70 — free max_monthly_orders = 200. client/src/components/SubscriptionPanel.tsx:118-131 — the merchant's bar shows used/limit with the label «طلبات الشهر» (entitlement.service.ts:31) and no breakdown. Merchant recourse: SubscriptionPanel.tsx:45-64 → platform.ts:294 → owner approval only (entitlement.service.ts:521-559).

**العلاج المقترح:** Exclude terminal non-sales from the meter in entitlement.service.ts:79: add `status: { notIn: ["cancelled", "rejected"] }` (returned orders were real deliveries and may stay counted, matching ق-135's «money that passed through the platform» definition). Record the rule beside ق-135/ق-136 in the ledger and in FEATURE_LABELS or the panel text («لا تُحسب الطلبات الملغاة والمرفوضة»). Add a phase16 test: at 200/200 with one rejected order, the next checkout succeeds.

### 16-20-11 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/finance.service.ts:330`

**الادّعاء:** A settlement pays only the owner's chosen date range, but the merchant's wallet then jumps to that settlement's period_to, so every ledger entry before period_from or between two settlements is silently dropped from «المستحق لك» without ever being paid — and the immutable settlement row cannot be repaired.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant who registered on 20 August and sold through August sees the wallet card say «منذ بداية نشاطك على المنصة — لم تُسجَّل لك تسوية بعد» with his full balance (client/src/components/MerchantWalletCard.tsx:64). The owner opens the finance center in September; its date pickers default to the first of the current month (client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:47-48) and «تسوية الفترة» sends exactly that range (FinanceCenter.tsx:75). The settlement pays September only; the merchant's card now reads 0 and «إجمالي ما استُلم» shows the smaller figure. August's sales are in no settlement and never will be: settlements_immutable (prisma/migrations/20260825190000_phase17_finance_domains_registration/migration.sql:88-90) forbids editing or deleting the row, and createSettlement refuses any later period starting before its period_to (finance.service.ts:330-331). The same mechanism bites monthly: a settlement made on 30 Sep at 14:00 has period_to clamped to 14:00 (finance.service.ts:172); next month the picker defaults to 1 Oct, so ten hours of 30 Sep sales are lost; if the owner instead settled on 3 Oct with the default `to`=today, November's default `from`=1 Nov drops 3 Oct→1 Nov — a month of a merchant's cash-on-delivery takings. The owner cannot see the discrepancy because the merchant's pending figure is a merchant-session-only read (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/platform.ts:192-195) and his own screen is a range-bound report (finance.service.ts:267-274), never an «unsettled since last payout» figure. The debt register records only the one-second version of this gap as D-93 (docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:335-346).

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1. client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:47-48 — `from` defaults to `Date.UTC(year, month, 1)`, `to` to today; nothing in the component reads the merchant's last settlement (openHistory at :85-99 only lists rows for display). 2. FinanceCenter.tsx:75 — POST /api/platform/finance/settlements with periodFrom `${from}T00:00:00Z`, periodTo `${to}T23:59:59Z`. 3. phase1-backend/src/http/routes/platform.ts:205-209 → finance.service.ts:303 createSettlement; :305 settlementPeriod clamps `to` to now (:172). 4. finance.service.ts:325-332 — the only guard: `if (previous && from < previous.periodTo) throw 409`; a `from` AFTER previous.periodTo (a gap) or a first settlement whose `from` is after the merchant's earliest entries passes. The comment at :319-320 claims «The rule is CONTIGUITY» but the code enforces non-overlap only. 5. finance.service.ts:333-336 — totals are summed over `createdAt: { gte: from, lte: to }` only; :338-353 writes the row. 6. phase1-backend/src/services/merchant-wallet.service.ts:85-91 — `from = lastSettlement.periodTo` (latest period_to, regardless of its period_from); :94-96 pending = entries `createdAt gt from`; :105 the card's window. So entries outside every settled range vanish from pending the moment any later settlement exists. 7. client/src/components/MerchantWalletCard.tsx:57,64,88 — the merchant reads «المستحق لك», the window sentence, and «إجمالي ما استُلم» from exactly these fields. 8. The acceptance test asserts «the settlement pays exactly what the wallet said was pending» (phase1-backend/tests/phase17.finance.integration.ts:203) but its fromIso is 30 days back (:153) and the oldest fixture entry is 16 days back (:130), so the equality holds by fixture accident; :206 then asserts the window jump that is the loss mechanism.

**العلاج المقترح:** In createSettlement (finance.service.ts:325-336) enforce contiguity rather than non-overlap: when a previous settlement exists, ignore the client's periodFrom and use `previous.periodTo` (or refuse with 422 if it differs); when none exists, sum from the beginning (`createdAt: { lte: to }` with periodFrom = the merchant's first ledger entry). Use `lt: to` with periodTo at the next midnight to close D-93 in the same change. In FinanceCenter.tsx prefill and lock «من» from the merchant's last settlement (or «منذ البداية») and show the pending net computed by merchantWallet — expose merchantWallet(merchantId) to `finance.view` actors so the owner settles the figure the merchant is looking at. Add to the phase17 suite a fixture entry created before fromIso and assert the settlement plus the post-settlement wallet still account for it.

### 16-20-12 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/finance.service.ts:330`

**الادّعاء:** Settlement contiguity is one-sided (no overlap, but gaps allowed), so ledger entries between a clamped period end and the next period's midnight start are never settled and disappear from the merchant's wallet

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant is silently never paid for orders completed after the owner's settle-click and before the next period's start. On the UI's default path the loss is weeks, not seconds: FinanceCenter defaults from=1st of month, to=today (client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:47-48) and settles exactly that range (:75). Owner settles Sep 1–«today» on Sep 10 at 14:32 Baghdad → periodTo is clamped to now = 2026-09-10T11:32:07Z. In October the form defaults to Oct 1–Oct 5; the guard accepts it (Oct 1 ≥ Sep 10 11:32); every completed order from Sep 10 11:32Z to Oct 1 00:00Z is in neither settlement. Before that second settlement the wallet card told the merchant «مستحقاتك غير المسوّاة حتى الآن — هذا ما ستتضمنه تسويتك القادمة» (MerchantWalletCard.tsx:61) including those orders; after it, the pending window reopens at Oct 5 and they vanish from «محفظتي», from «آخر تسوية» and from «إجمالي ما استُلم». The gap can never be back-filled in-app: the guard compares only against the LATEST periodTo, so a later settlement for [Sep 10 11:32, Oct 1) is refused as SETTLEMENT_PERIOD_OVERLAP although it overlaps nothing, and settlements_immutable forbids editing. Even a disciplined owner who obeys the 409 text «ابدأ الفترة الجديدة من ذلك التاريخ أو بعده» hits it: the form can only send that date as T00:00:00Z, which is < the mid-day periodTo → 409 again → he picks the next day → the rest of that day's orders (12+ hours, ~3 AM Baghdad boundary) are lost the same way.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1) Client sends date-only bounds: FinanceCenter.tsx:75 `periodFrom: `${from}T00:00:00Z`, periodTo: `${to}T23:59:59Z``; defaults :47-48 (first of month / today) shared with the report at :61. 2) Period end clamped to now: finance.service.ts:170-174 `const to = requested > now ? now : requested`, `now` taken at :305 — the comment at :163-166 states «حتى اليوم» is the ordinary operator action, so periodTo is mid-day with ms precision. 3) Guard is one-sided: :325-332 reads only the latest settlement (orderBy periodTo desc) and throws 409 only when `from < previous.periodTo`; any `from > previous.periodTo` is accepted (the message at :331 says «أو بعده»). 4) Ledger read is bounded to the client's from: :333-336 `createdAt: { gte: from, lte: to }` — entries between previous.periodTo and from are not summed into :338-353. 5) Wallet hides them afterwards: merchant-wallet.service.ts:85-91 `from = lastSettlement?.periodTo` (latest periodTo), :96 `createdAt: { gt: from, lte: now }`; lifetime :100/:108 sums settlement components only. 6) No back-fill possible: :325-330 compares only against the latest periodTo; the DB exclusion constraint (prisma/migrations/20260904142000_rt_money_settlement_no_overlap/migration.sql:46-47) would allow the gap settlement but the service refuses first; settlements_immutable (20260825190000_phase17_finance_domains_registration/migration.sql:88-90) forbids repair. 7) The suite never exercises a gap: phase17.finance.integration.ts:248 passes `periodFrom: walletSettled.periodTo` (exact ms ISO the UI cannot produce); :206 asserts only that the wallet window starts at periodTo.

**العلاج المقترح:** In createSettlement, when `previous` exists ignore the client's periodFrom and snap `from = previous.periodTo`, reading the ledger with `createdAt: { gt: from, lte: to }` (matching the wallet's `gt` at merchant-wallet.service.ts:96 so an entry at exactly periodTo is not double-counted); keep `gte` only for a merchant's first settlement. Optionally also refuse `from > previous.periodTo` with a 409 that names the exact ISO instant, and have FinanceCenter pre-fill the settle form's start from `lastSettlement.periodTo` verbatim rather than a date-only picker.

### 16-20-13 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/finance.service.ts:330`

**الادّعاء:** The owner's default settle flow leaves a gap between settlements, and the merchant's money in that gap disappears from every screen without ever being paid

**الأثر المزعوم:** Ordinary cadence: the owner settles a merchant on 20 Sept (the form's `to` is today), opens the same page on 15 Oct where `from` defaults to 1 Oct, and settles again. Entries from 21–30 Sept are in no settlement. The merchant's wallet had said «مستحقاتك غير المسوّاة حتى الآن — هذا ما ستتضمنه تسويتك القادمة» for the whole span since 20 Sept; after the second settlement the pending window opens at 15 Oct, «إجمالي ما استُلم» is the sum of the two settlements, and the ten days of cash-on-delivery sales are visible nowhere as unpaid. A merchant doing 30 orders a week loses a week and a half of net payout per cycle this happens, silently, on an append-only record nobody can amend.

**الدليل المزعوم:** createSettlement's only period guard refuses `from < previous.periodTo` (finance.service.ts:325-332) — a later `from` passes, and the 19-د comment at :320-324 reasons about overlap only. The owner's screen seeds `from` to the first of the current month and `to` to today (client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:47-48) and sends exactly that range as the settlement period (FinanceCenter.tsx:75); the confirm form shows a note box and nothing about the merchant's last settled date (FinanceCenter.tsx:160-166). The wallet then opens the pending window at the LATEST `periodTo` (merchant-wallet.service.ts:85-91) and counts only `createdAt > from` (merchant-wallet.service.ts:96), so entries before the new `periodFrom` fall out of pending; lifetime is the sum of settlement rows (merchant-wallet.service.ts:100,108). The card promises the merchant the pending figure is what his next settlement will contain (client/src/components/MerchantWalletCard.tsx:61). The suite only ever settles contiguously — `periodFrom: walletSettled.periodTo` (phase1-backend/tests/phase17.finance.integration.ts:248) — so a gap is never exercised.

**العلاج المقترح:** In createSettlement, treat a gap like an overlap: after the `previous` lookup, if `previous && from.valueOf() !== previous.periodTo.valueOf()` throw 409 «يجب أن تبدأ الفترة الجديدة من نهاية آخر تسوية (YYYY-MM-DD) بالضبط.» (or, for a first settlement, from the merchant's start). Have FinanceCenter send `periodFrom` = that merchant's last `periodTo` rather than the report's `from` — expose `lastSettledTo` on each row of platformFinanceReport (one extra `settlement.findFirst` per merchant) so the form can prefill and print it.

### 16-20-14 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/finance.service.ts:172`

**الادّعاء:** createSettlement clamps periodTo to now() while the next settlement is allowed to start at the operator's next-day midnight, so every ledger entry written between the settle click and midnight is never paid and vanishes from every screen after the following settlement

**الأثر المزعوم:** The merchant. The owner's default flow is exactly the losing one: FinanceCenter defaults «إلى» to today (client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:48) and posts `periodTo: ${to}T23:59:59Z` (:75); the service clamps that to `now` (finance.service.ts:172) — the code's own comment calls this the ordinary case («حتى اليوم» is «genuinely in the future for most of the working day», :163-165). The stored `periodTo` is therefore e.g. 2026-09-10T11:00:00Z. The contiguity guard (:330) refuses any `from < previous.periodTo`, and its message names only the DATE («تنتهي في 2026-09-10. ابدأ الفترة الجديدة من ذلك التاريخ أو بعده», :331), so the operator who obeys it sends 2026-09-10T00:00:00Z, is refused again, and moves to 2026-09-11T00:00:00Z — which passes both the guard and the `[from,to)` exclusion constraint (prisma/migrations/20260904142000_rt_money_settlement_no_overlap/migration.sql:46-47). Every `order_completed` entry from 11:00 to 24:00 on 09-10 — in Iraq that is the evening, when the courier hands back cash and the merchant walks the day's COD orders to `completed` (order-lifecycle.service.ts:271-287 books the ledger row then) — is in no settlement. Until the next settlement the wallet still shows them as pending (merchant-wallet.service.ts:91,96 `createdAt > lastSettlement.periodTo`); after it, the window moves past them and they leave the wallet too, while `lifetime.settledNetIqd` sums only settlement rows (:100,108). `settlements_immutable` (20260825190000_phase17…/migration.sql:88-90) means no in-app repair. A platform that settles weekly at 11:00 quietly withholds every same-day-evening completion from every merchant, forever; the same hole also swallows `order_returned` entries, so clawbacks in the gap are never taken either. Narrower but same root: a completion transaction whose `createdAt` (Prisma `@default(now())`, schema.prisma:1279) precedes the settlement read but commits after it is invisible to the settlement and then excluded by the next `from` — a cutoff by wall-clock with no settled marker on the ledger.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Route: platform.ts:207-210 → `createSettlement(actor, body)`. finance.service.ts:305 `settlementPeriod(body, new Date())` → :171 parses from/to → :172 `to = requested > now ? now : requested` → :173 refuses only `to <= from`. :325-329 reads the latest previous settlement by `periodTo desc`; :330 `if (previous && from < previous.periodTo)` → 409 (the comment at :319-324 states the rule is «CONTIGUITY… `>=` rather than `>`», i.e. gaps are permitted by design). :333-337 selects `createdAt: { gte: from, lte: to }` and sums in memory (`totalsFromEntries`, :178-189); :338-353 writes `periodFrom: from, periodTo: to` (the clamped value). Wallet: merchant-wallet.service.ts:85-91 `from = lastSettlement.periodTo`; :96 `createdAt: { gt: from, lte: now }`. Client: FinanceCenter.tsx:46-48 defaults `to` to today; :75 posts `T23:59:59Z`. Test coverage: phase17.finance.integration.ts:153-154 uses `toIso = now + 60s` and :248-250 only asserts `periodTo <= Date.now()` — the gap is never exercised. REGISTER HONESTY on the sampled rows this touches: D-93 (docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:335-346) is MIS-RATED — it describes a one-second `lte` boundary and rates it Low, while the clamp at :172 on the default UI path opens up to a full day; its proposed fix («حدّ أعلى حصريّ lt مع periodTo عند منتصف الليل التالي») cannot close this because :172 still cuts at `now`. D-51 (register :77, and still listed OPEN under «المرحلة 20» at :167) is SILENTLY FIXED in code (migration 20260904142000 + finance.service.ts:325-332) — the row was never updated; D-94 (:348-358) is accurate (the migration returns without the constraint when overlaps pre-exist, migration.sql:41-44); D-16 (:122) is honestly half-open — :333-337 still pulls every entry of the period into memory. Other sampled rows: D-46 closed as claimed (20260904100000_d46_reservation_expiry + reservation-expiry-job.ts:116-155); D-80 (:182) silently fixed (coupon.service.ts:96 carries `AND deleted_at IS NULL`) but not marked; D-47 (:73, listed open at :167) half silently fixed — P2028 is now retried (db/serializable-retry.ts:46) while the ~20-trip transaction stands; D-17 (:43, listed open at :167) silently fixed by ق-222 (storefront.service.ts:837-867 runs the plan check before any lock); D-48 (:74, marked 🧭 scheduled at :156) silently fixed (product-catalog.service.ts:429-431 → softDelete); D-85 (:198) still open exactly as written (storefront.service.ts:460-468 reads the variant before `$transaction`).

**العلاج المقترح:** Stop trusting the client for continuity: in `createSettlement`, when a previous settlement exists set `from = previous.periodTo` (refuse a client `periodFrom` that differs, or ignore it) and select `createdAt: { gt: from, lte: to }` — the wallet's own predicate (merchant-wallet.service.ts:96) — so consecutive windows tile exactly and the clamp becomes harmless; this also closes D-93's boundary. Keep the clamp for `to`. Durable form: a `settlement_entries(ledger_entry_id UNIQUE, settlement_id)` table written in the same transaction, with the settlement selecting «entries of this merchant with no settlement_entries row and createdAt <= now» — settling by identity, not by wall-clock, which also removes the in-flight-commit hole.

### 16-20-15 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:52`

**الادّعاء:** Guest checkout issues a live customer session (and returns the account email) for any pre-existing guest customer row matched only by phone number, giving a stranger another shopper's orders, addresses, prescriptions, and the ability to lock them out

**الأثر المزعوم:** In this cash-on-delivery market a phone number is semi-public (printed on parcels, known to couriers, shared by a neighbourhood). ق-201 makes registration optional, so the DEFAULT shopper is a guest row with a null password; createManualCustomer (manual-provisioning.service.ts:137) also stamps isGuest:true on every hand-added customer with the merchant's real phone/name. Anyone who knows such a phone can add one cart item, POST /store/:slug/checkout/guest with {phone:<victim>}, and receive (a) a working customer-session cookie bound to the victim's row and (b) the victim's email in the 201 body. With that session they read GET /account/orders and /account/orders/:orderId (full customerName, recipientPhone, shippingAddressLine1/2, governorate, notes) and /account/orders/:orderId/prescription (medical images). Then POST /account/password (setGuestPassword) sets the attacker's password on the row (isGuest→false), permanently locking the real shopper out of ever claiming their own orders. This is precisely the identity-isolation break the register's PHASE18_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md 2.1/3.6 says does not exist, and it is unlisted in the register.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:200-213 — POST /store/:slug/checkout/guest calls guestCustomerFor(store.id, body), then createTypedSession("customer", customer.id), setCustomerSession(response, slug, token), and responds {order, account: customer, accessToken}. guest-checkout.service.ts:51-57 — existing = customer.findFirst({where:{storeId, phoneNormalized}}); line 52 refuses ONLY existing && !existing.isGuest (password accounts); an existing GUEST row is updated and RETURNED (GUEST_SELECT includes id/email/phoneNormalized) with no proof the caller controls the phone. auth.service.ts issues guest rows that accumulate real orders; a guest row is active, so shared.ts:228-234 requireStorefrontCustomer (customer.findFirst({id: claims.subjectId, storeId, isActive:true})) resolves the attacker's session to the victim row. storefront.service.ts:1049-1053 customerOrder returns the full StoreOrder (address, recipientPhone, customerName, note). storefront.ts:215-220 → auth.service.ts:276-283 setGuestPassword flips isGuest→false, sealing the takeover. No OTP/phone-verification gate exists (phoneVerifiedAt is never set on this path).

**العلاج المقترح:** Do not bind a session to a pre-existing customer row that the caller has not proven they control. On guest checkout, either (a) require phone-ownership proof (an OTP — the deferred D-05 confirmation flow) before reusing/returning an existing guest row, or (b) refuse a phone that already has a guest row the same way an account phone is refused, forcing the real owner to claim it via registration; and never return the reused row's `account` (email) or a session token when the row pre-existed. At minimum gate reuse on a set phoneVerifiedAt.

### 16-20-16 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:52`

**الادّعاء:** A shopper who registered with only his phone and forgot his password can never buy from that shop again: the reset door asks for an email he never gave, the guest door refuses his number, re-registering answers in English, and the merchant he is told to contact has no tool to help him.

**الأثر المزعوم:** Every Iraqi shopper is phone-first and email is optional at registration by design. The moment such a shopper forgets his password, that phone number is dead at that store: he cannot sign in, cannot reset, cannot check out as a guest (409 «هذا الرقم مسجّل لدينا — سجّل الدخول لإتمام الطلب»), cannot re-register (an English 409), and the screen's only advice — «تواصل مع المتجر» — sends him to a merchant who has no reset, unlink or delete action. The merchant loses a returning customer (his most valuable kind in COD) and a WhatsApp argument he cannot win; the platform loses the order. The register's D-12 row still says «OTP الزبون → 19-أ» although phase 19 is closed and PR-1 shipped email-only, and the ق-201 interaction that turned «cannot log in» into «cannot buy» is not registered anywhere.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Registration is phone-keyed with email optional: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:114-125 → phase1-backend/src/http/schemas/storefront.schema.ts:67-76 («`email` is genuinely optional») → phase1-backend/src/auth/auth.service.ts:250-268 (`normalizeOptionalEmail`, identity = `phoneNormalized`). Login is phone+password: storefront.ts:126-137 → auth.service.ts:286-299. The forgot link client/src/pages/CustomerAuthPage.tsx:85 («هل نسيت كلمة السر؟») opens client/src/pages/PasswordResetRequest.tsx:98-115, whose only field is `<Input type="email" required>` (:114) and whose small print reads «إن لم تكن قد سجّلت بريداً إلكترونياً في هذا المتجر، تواصل مع المتجر لاستعادة حسابك.» (:108); it POSTs to storefront.ts:148-153, whose own comment (:145-147) admits «this can only reach a shopper who left one»; phase1-backend/src/services/password-reset.service.ts:224-251 resolves a customer by `{ storeId, email, isActive }` only (:251) and the only channel is `channel: "email"` (:482-483). Guest checkout refuses his phone: guest-checkout.service.ts:51-52 (`existing && !existing.isGuest` → 409 CUSTOMER_ACCOUNT_EXISTS), rendered by client/src/pages/StorefrontCheckoutPage.tsx:242,267 as a link «سجّل الدخول بهذا الرقم لإتمام الطلب» back to the door he cannot pass. Re-registering claims only `isGuest: true` rows (auth.service.ts:260-267); a password row falls to `customer.create` (:268) → P2002 → phase1-backend/src/http/errors.ts:201-206 → 409 DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_PHONE "This phone is already registered in this store." (English); client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:1-51 has no such code so `errorMessage` (:373) prints the English sentence at CustomerAuthPage.tsx:63,75. The merchant's customer routes are create / block-unblock / list only (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/merchant.ts:67-93); manual creation refuses a known phone with CUSTOMER_PHONE_TAKEN (phase1-backend/src/services/manual-provisioning.service.ts:119-125); nothing in customer-directory.service.ts touches `passwordHash` (grep), and password.service.ts:75-105 changes only the caller's own password. Register: docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:142 (D-12 remaining «OTP الزبون → 19-أ») vs docs/PROJECT_STATE.md:22,29 (phase 19 done, PR-1 shipped); docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:300 (ق-201 rule «هاتفٌ يعود لحسابٍ يُرفَض كضيف (409)») with no follow-on row for the locked-out case.

**العلاج المقترح:** Smallest: give the merchant a rescue lever — a `POST /merchant/stores/:storeSlug/customers/:customerId/reset-access` (settings.manage) that sets `passwordHash = null, isGuest = true` and revokes the customer's sessions; auth.service.ts:260-267 then lets the shopper re-claim his own row (orders, addresses, points) on his next register, and guest-checkout.service.ts:52 stops refusing him. Show that action in the directory row and change PasswordResetRequest.tsx:108 to say what the merchant can do.
