Proper: a phone-channel one-time code on the store-scoped reset route (the WhatsApp-manual path already exists), and map DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_PHONE to Arabic in storefront-api.ts. Register: split D-12 into shipped/unshipped halves with file:line, and add the ق-201 dead end as its own row.

### 16-20-17 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:55`

**الادّعاء:** Guest checkout and registration hand a live customer session for an existing guest row to anyone who types that row's phone number — no proof of possession — exposing the previous shopper's order history, home address, prescription image and loyalty points, and letting the caller lock him out

**الأثر المزعوم:** In this market the phone number is printed on every COD parcel, shared in every WhatsApp thread and known to every courier and neighbour. Anyone holding it can, at any store where the victim bought as a guest (guest checkout is ON by default, prisma/schema.prisma:192): read his full order history and items, his saved delivery address, and — on a pharmacy store — the photograph of his prescription; spend his loyalty points on the attacker's own order; upload a replacement prescription onto his pending pharmacy order; overwrite his name/email in the merchant's directory; and set a password on the row so the real owner is refused forever («هذا الرقم مسجّل لدينا — سجّل الدخول لإتمام الطلب») and cannot register again because the phone is unique per store. The merchant's own hand-typed customer book (ق-212 rows are guest rows) is claimable the same way. Cost: one unauthenticated POST per victim; 40 per IP per store per 10 minutes.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Door 1, guest checkout: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:200-213 — :205 limiter (12 per caller-chosen guest cookie, 40 per IP), :206 parse, :207 guestCustomerFor(store.id, body), :208 createTypedSession("customer", customer.id), :209 setCustomerSession (cookie), :210 guestCheckout, :212 JSON body returns accessToken. phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:44 only checks the store switch (storefront-config.service.ts:387-390; default true at prisma/schema.prisma:192); :51 findFirst({storeId, phoneNormalized}); :52 refuses ONLY a non-guest row; :55-57 an existing GUEST row is reused and its fullName/phone/email overwritten with the caller's values. No possession check anywhere: customers.phoneVerifiedAt exists (prisma/schema.prisma:307) but has no writer in phase1-backend/src (grep empty). identity.ts:185-216 mints a customer_sessions row for subjectId = existing.id; shared.ts:228-234 requireStorefrontCustomer accepts it on id+storeId+isActive alone. No cart is needed: guest-checkout.service.ts:84-87 throws CART_EMPTY only after the session exists, and errors.ts:30-39 answers response.status().json() without touching headers, so the Set-Cookie from shared.ts:71-73 and the DB session survive the 422. What that session reaches: storefront.ts:221-222 → storefront.service.ts:1031-1052 customerOrders/customerOrder keyed on (storeId, customerId); storefront.ts:162 → storefront.service.ts:574-576 saved addresses (every guest order stores one, storefront.service.ts:624); storefront.ts:232-236 → prescription.service.ts:95-99 via ownedOrder :28-33 streams the prescription image; storefront.ts:227-231 → prescription.service.ts:36-41 accepts a new upload while pending_upload/rejected; storefront.ts:167-195 regular checkout with loyaltyPoints → storefront.service.ts:981-987 → loyalty.service.ts:391-409 reads the holding by customerId; storefront.ts:215-219 → auth.service.ts:279-287 setGuestPassword flips isGuest=false under the attacker's password. Lockout: guest-checkout.service.ts:52 then refuses the real owner; auth.service.ts:263 finds no guest row and :271 create hits customers_store_phone_present_uq (prisma/migrations/20260818011500_storefront_customer_carts_reservations_9a/migration.sql:13) → errors.ts:206 DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_PHONE. Door 2, registration (no cart, no guest switch, 8 per IP at storefront.ts:117): storefront.ts:114-125 → auth.service.ts:263 claims any isGuest:true row by phone and :270 sets the caller's password on it in the same write; manual-provisioning.service.ts:137 makes every merchant-added directory row isGuest:true (ledger ق-212, docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:311), so those are claimable too. The ledger row for ق-201 (docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:300) records three rules and none of them is «prove the phone is yours».

**العلاج المقترح:** Treat a pre-existing guest row as somebody else's until the phone is proven. (1) guest-checkout.service.ts:55-57 — do not reuse/overwrite an existing row; refuse it with the same 409 as an account phone until an OTP step (SMS/WhatsApp) stamps customers.phoneVerifiedAt, which the schema already carries. (2) auth.service.ts:263-270 — same rule: registerCustomer may claim a guest row only after phoneVerifiedAt is set for that phone in this request. (3) storefront.ts:208-209 — move createTypedSession/setCustomerSession to after guestCheckout succeeds, so a failed request never leaves a session behind, and issue it only for a row created in this request. (4) storefront.ts:215 setGuestPassword — require the same verification. Add a two-store test that claims store A's guest row from a second client and asserts 404 on /account/orders and /prescription.

### 16-20-18 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/list-page.ts:83`

**الادّعاء:** The house keyset cursor carries the ordering timestamp at millisecond precision while every ordered column is timestamptz(6), so ASC lists repeat the boundary row on page 2 and DESC lists silently skip same-millisecond neighbours.

**الأثر المزعوم:** Deterministic on the three ASC keyset lists: the merchant's returns queue (the refused-at-the-door queue that is the normal Iraqi return), the owner's pending-registration queue, and an order's notes. Page 2 begins with the last row of page 1 whenever that row's created_at has a non-zero microsecond fraction — which is essentially every row minted by Prisma's now() in this database. Latent on every DESC list: rows written within the same millisecond as a page's last row (a CSV import loop inside one transaction, csv-transfer.service.ts:319; a loyaltyEntry.createMany batch, loyalty.service.ts:304/348 → paged at :466) are dropped from the walk with no error, no gap marker and a closing nextCursor:null that asserts completeness. This is the exact defect the phase introduced keyset paging to eliminate (list-page.ts:159-165), and every suite is blind to it because rows are seeded one HTTP request apart or with an explicit millisecond createdAt.

**الدليل المزعوم:** encodeListCursor serialises a Date with value.toISOString() — millisecond precision (list-page.ts:83). readListCursor/decodeListCursor hand the string to keysetWhere, which rebuilds it with new Date(cursor.value) (list-page.ts:213) and emits {field: {lte|gte: value}} AND {OR: [{field: {lt|gt: value}}, {id: …}]} (list-page.ts:216-226) — the comparison runs in PostgreSQL against the full-precision column. Every keyset ordering column is declared @default(now()) … @db.Timestamptz(6) (e.g. Product.createdAt, prisma/schema.prisma:513; migration DDL `created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()`, 20260817093000/migration.sql:12). Proof that a Prisma create on such a column lands with microseconds in this database is already in the repo: notificationOutbox.nextAttemptAt is the same declaration (schema.prisma:1133) and pg3.integration.mjs:107-110 records the JS Date reading `.494000` for a stored `.494832` — i.e. Prisma truncates to the millisecond. Walk it through the ASC lists: listStoreReturnRequests orders [{createdAt:'asc'},{id:'asc'}] and keysets keysetWhere('createdAt','asc') (return-request.service.ts:140,149); with the boundary row at .494832 the cursor holds .494000, and the page-2 predicate created_at >= .494000 AND (created_at > .494000 OR id > L.id) re-admits the boundary row. Same shape at order-notes.service.ts:74,76 and registration.service.ts:116. On DESC lists (catalog: product-catalog.service.ts:261-267) the predicate created_at <= .494000 excludes every row in (.494000, .494832] that sorted after the boundary row. Why CI is green: contract.list-envelope.integration.mjs walks only DESC or text-keyed lists (:175-217); its products are created by HTTP POST one request apart (:144-148) and its orders carry an explicit millisecond createdAt (:167); ps1.platform-staff.integration.mjs:567-579 and inventory-movements.integration.mjs:113-123 likewise seed rows one request apart; no suite pages any of the three ASC lists (grep for registration-requests|/returns|/notes with limit/cursor: none). keyset-index.guard.mjs checks index shape and orderBy agreement only, never precision.

**العلاج المقترح:** Make the stored precision equal what the cursor can carry: one migration `ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN created_at TYPE timestamptz(3)` (and the other keyset ordering columns: deleted_at, returned_at, inspected_at, last_activity_at) with the matching `@db.Timestamptz(3)` in schema.prisma, so the value PostgreSQL compares is byte-identical to the value the cursor round-trips. Then add a same-millisecond fixture to contract.list-envelope.integration.mjs (rows inserted with an identical or microsecond-apart created_at straddling a page boundary) and a walk over one ASC list, so the ratchet actually tests the tiebreaker it exists for.

### 16-20-19 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/order-lifecycle.service.ts:303`

**الادّعاء:** A return marked after the 15-day window silently keeps the commission booked and the sale in the merchant's pending payout; nobody is told and the owner's only remedy is a text box demanding an order UUID no screen shows

**الأثر المزعوم:** In Iraq the courier brings refused and returned parcels back in batches, so a merchant marking «مرتجع» on day 16 or 20 after completion is the normal case, not the edge. He gets a green success, the order reads مرتجع, and the ledger does nothing: his wallet keeps counting the sale (subtotal + delivery − commission) as owed to him for goods that came back, and the platform keeps its commission on a sale that was refunded at the door. Whichever side the cash sits on, the next settlement pays or charges for a sale that did not happen, and neither party has a screen that says so. The owner can only fix it if he already knows which order it was and pastes its UUID.

**الدليل المزعوم:** The state machine allows completed→returned at any age (order-lifecycle.service.ts:14-19) and the merchant's order screen mirrors it (client/src/components/OrderWorkspace.tsx:82). transitionOrderStatus calls recordOrderReturn and discards its result (order-lifecycle.service.ts:302-303); recordOrderReturn returns "outside_window" without writing anything (finance.service.ts:79-81); the transition answers with the plain order payload carrying no ledger information (order-lifecycle.service.ts:330-332). The deadline is surfaced only on the shopper-initiated return path (client/src/components/ReturnsQueue.tsx:137, CustomerReturnsPage.tsx:114) — no `reversalDeadline` or window text exists anywhere in OrderWorkspace. The wallet keeps summing the completion entry as owed (merchant-wallet.service.ts:94-107). The owner's remedy is a box labelled «معرّف الطلب (UUID)» (client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:186-190); the platform order list prints `orderNumber` only (OrderWorkspace.tsx:381), and platformFinanceReport has no view of «returned but not reversed» (finance.service.ts:267-295). The ADR chose the window (docs/adr/ADR-006:5-11) but the silence is the code's.

**العلاج المقترح:** Capture the outcome in transitionOrderStatus (`const reversal = await recordOrderReturn(...)`) and, when it is "outside_window", stamp it on the status event note and return it in the payload so OrderWorkspace shows «انتهت مهلة العكس التلقائي (15 يوماً) — العكس المالي لهذا الطلب يسجَّل بتسوية يدوية من إدارة المنصة». Give the owner a list instead of a UUID box: one query for orders with status `returned` that have an `order_completed` entry and no `order_returned`/`manual_adjustment` entry, rendered in FinanceCenter with a per-row «تسجيل قيد الإرجاع» calling the existing adjustments endpoint.

### 16-20-20 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/owner-account.service.ts:68`

**الادّعاء:** Owner email change is the direct password-only update ق-48 explicitly forbade: no confirmation from either address, no session revocation, no security notice, and no attempt ceiling on its password check

**الأثر المزعوم:** Whoever holds the owner's password and a live session (phished password with TOTP off — the default, see finding 3 — or a borrowed unlocked laptop plus a shoulder-surfed password) re-points the only account with no tenant boundary above it in one request: after the write, password-reset links resolve by the NEW address, so the real owner cannot recover, his other sessions keep running for the attacker, and nobody is told. The ledger itself calls this «ثغرة استيلاء على الحساب». The password compare on this route also has none of the 19-د re-auth ceiling that the other two password gates got, so a session holder can grind the password here without a 429.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Promise: docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:95 (ق-48): «تدفق تغيير بريد آمن لحساب المالك بتأكيد مزدوج من العنوانين + إبطال الجلسات + إشعار أمني (لا تحديث مباشر — تلك ثغرة استيلاء على الحساب)»; no later ledger entry re-scopes it (grep for PC-1/تغيير البريد in the ledger returns nothing). Code: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/owner-security.ts:71-78 — `requirePlatformOwner` then `changeOwnerEmail`, comment at :75-76 «The session is NOT revoked». Service owner-account.service.ts:51-85: `verifyPassword` (:55) with no `consumeStorefrontRateLimit` (the file imports none; contrast password.service.ts:82-83 and owner-totp.service.ts:143-144, and storefront.service.ts:203-213 which names only those two gates as ceilinged), `normalizeEmail` (:61), then `tx.platformOwner.update({ data: { email } })` (:71) plus an audit row (:72-81) — no token, no confirmation from old or new address, no `revokeAllSessionsWithin`, and the only consumer of `owner_email_changed` is the audit list (grep: platform-audit.service.ts:64, owner-account.service.ts:76 — no outbox row, so no notice). Header comment :17-21 states the design choice «THE SESSION SURVIVES». Consequence path: password-reset.service.ts:227-229 resolves a platform_owner by `email`, so recovery follows the attacker's address immediately.

**العلاج المقترح:** Implement ق-48 as written: on request, mint two single-use timed tokens (reuse the password_reset_tokens table/`sealSecret` outbox pattern), mail one to the old and one to the new address, and only write `email` inside a transaction that also calls `revokeAllSessionsWithin(tx, "platform_owner", ownerId, now)` and queues a security notice to both addresses. Immediately, add the REAUTH ceiling around the `verifyPassword` at :55 exactly as password.service.ts:82-91 does.

### 16-20-21 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/owner-account.service.ts:71`

**الادّعاء:** The owner's sign-in email is rewritten in one direct UPDATE with no proof of possession, no session revocation and no invalidation of live reset tokens, which ق-48 explicitly forbade as an account-takeover hole

**الأثر المزعوم:** The platform-owner account, the one identity with nothing above it. A mistyped or attacker-chosen address becomes the recovery address of the root account instantly: whoever holds that mailbox requests a reset by the CURRENT email (password-reset.service.ts:227-229), consumes it (writes the hash and revokes every owner session, 583-605) and signs in; the code permits the owner to have no second factor at all (auth.service.ts:102-122, disableTotp at owner-totp.service.ts:134-156), and ق-150 records production ran a month with 2FA impossible to enable. In the other direction, an owner who changes his email BECAUSE the old mailbox was compromised leaves every reset link already mailed to that mailbox live for its remaining 45 minutes — consume never compares the token's emailNormalized with the account's current address (566-570). Sessions survive the change by design (owner-security.ts:75-76) and the deviation is asserted in tests/pc1.platform-contact.integration.mjs:281, so nothing will catch it.

**الدليل المزعوم:** docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:95 — ق-48: «تدفق تغيير بريد آمن لحساب المالك بتأكيد مزدوج من العنوانين + إبطال الجلسات + إشعار أمني (لا تحديث مباشر — تلك ثغرة استيلاء على الحساب)». Route: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/owner-security.ts:71-78 POST /platform/security/email → requirePlatformOwner → changeOwnerEmail; the comment at :75-76 says the session is NOT revoked. Service: owner-account.service.ts:52-59 verifies only the current password; :61-66 normalises and refuses the same address; :68-82 a transaction that does `tx.platformOwner.update({ where: { id: ownerId }, data: { email } })` (:71) plus an audit row (:72-81) — no confirmation token minted for either address, no outbox row, no revokeAllSessionsWithin, no invalidateLiveResetTokens (its only callers are password.service.ts:121 and password-reset.service.ts:365/464/599). The header :17-21 states the session deliberately survives. Downstream: password-reset.service.ts:227-229 resolves a platform_owner reset by the current email, so the new holder can mint a link; consume :566-570 selects no email and never checks it; :585-604 writes passwordHash and revokes ALL owner sessions. auth.service.ts:102-122 issues a session without a second factor when totpEnabledAt is null. tests/pc1.platform-contact.integration.mjs:281 asserts «the session he changed it from is still his».

**العلاج المقترح:** Implement ق-48 as written: `changeOwnerEmail` mints two single-use hashed tokens (reuse the password_reset_tokens shape with a new purpose) and queues a confirmation mail to BOTH addresses; only a consume carrying both proofs (or old-address confirm then new-address confirm) runs a transaction that updates `email`, calls `invalidateLiveResetTokens(tx, 'platform_owner', ownerId, 'email_changed', now)` and `revokeAllSessionsWithin(tx, 'platform_owner', ownerId, now)`, writes the audit row, and enqueues a security notice to both addresses. Until then, at minimum add the two invalidation calls to the existing transaction at owner-account.service.ts:68-82 and drop the pc1 assertion at :281.

### 16-20-22 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/owner-account.service.ts:71`

**الادّعاء:** Owner sign-in email is changed by a direct update with no confirmation to the old address and no session revocation, converting a password compromise of the root account into a permanent, silent takeover

**الأثر المزعوم:** The platform owner is the one identity with no tenant boundary above it and, by the code's own words, 'no support desk above it.' If an attacker learns the owner's password (phishing; and see finding 2 — owner 2FA is not forced), a single POST /platform/security/email permanently repoints the account's LOGIN identity. Login is keyed on email (loginPlatformOwner does platformOwner.findUnique({where:{email}}), auth.service.ts:93), so after the change the real owner's old email matches no row and he can no longer sign in OR self-recover (a reset would mail the attacker's new address). No security notice is sent to the OLD address, so the owner's first signal is being locked out of every merchant, customer and money surface on the platform. 19-أ's acceptance explicitly required the opposite: 'double confirmation from old and new address, all sessions revoked, security notice to both — direct update explicitly forbidden.' (Note: the code comments attribute this simplification to a later owner decision, PC-1, so the team should re-weigh rather than assume a bug.)

**الدليل المزعوم:** Route: owner-security.ts:71 app.post('/platform/security/email') -> requirePlatformOwner (:73) -> changeOwnerEmail (:77); the route comment at :74-76 states 'The session is NOT revoked and no other session is ended.' Service: owner-account.service.ts changeOwnerEmail reads the owner (:52), reauthenticates with verifyPassword (:55), normalizes the new email (:61), then in a transaction (:68) executes tx.platformOwner.update({where:{id:ownerId}, data:{email}}) (:71) — a direct write of the login identity — followed only by recordPlatformAuditEvent (:72-81). There is no revokeAllSessionsWithin/revokeOtherSessionsWithin call and no notificationOutbox insert to either the old or new address anywhere in the function. Contrast the sibling lifecycle acts that DO revoke in-transaction: password.service.ts:125 (revokeOtherSessionsWithin) and password-reset.service.ts:604 (revokeAllSessionsWithin).

**العلاج المقترح:** Restore the 19-أ design for this act: require confirmation tokens from BOTH the current and the new address before the email column is written (stage the pending change, confirm from old + new), send a security notice to the old address on completion, and revoke all owner sessions in the same transaction as the update — the way password.service.ts and password-reset.service.ts already revoke.

### 16-20-23 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/prescription.service.ts:51`

**الادّعاء:** A pharmacy shopper whose merchant is at the image-storage ceiling is told «قم بالترقية لرفع الوصفة» and his medicine order is stuck — the ق-216 audience split was applied to checkout and not to the sibling prescription path

**الأثر المزعوم:** The reader is a customer of a pharmacy store who already placed a COD order for a prescription product and now has to upload the prescription before the pharmacy may process it. He is answered with the merchant's plan invoice — «وصلت إلى حد «مساحة الصور (ميغابايت)» في خطة «المجانية» (200). قم بالترقية لرفع الوصفة.» — an instruction to upgrade a subscription he does not own, with no way forward. The order stays `pending_upload`, which the state machine refuses to move to processing, so the sale is lost and the shopper learns the shop is broken. This ceiling is met in a normal week, not an edge case: every new merchant spends his free month on growth (2048 MB) and lands on free (200 MB) on day 31, so any pharmacy that uploaded more than ~200 MB of product photos during the trial refuses EVERY prescription upload from that morning on, for every shopper, until the owner manually upgrades him. The repo's own checkout comment (storefront.ts:176) also names fake COD orders' prescription uploads as a known way to fill the merchant's storage.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:227-230 — customer-facing route POST /store/:slug/account/orders/:id/prescription calls uploadOrderPrescription. phase1-backend/src/services/prescription.service.ts:51 — assertWithinPlanLimit(prisma, merchantId, "max_image_storage_mb", …, "لرفع الوصفة") with no try/catch. phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:110-121 — throws 403 PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED with the merchant-voiced sentence `وصلت إلى حد «${label}» في خطة «${plan.name}» (${limit}). قم بالترقية ${action}.`. client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:333-373 — PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED is absent from the code→message map, so line 373 falls back to the raw server message. client/src/pages/CustomerOrderDetailPage.tsx:75-76,87 — setError(errorMessage(caught)) rendered under the upload button. phase1-backend/src/services/order-lifecycle.service.ts:253-254 — new→processing refused (PRESCRIPTION_NOT_APPROVED) while prescriptionStatus !== approved, so the order is stuck. Contrast: phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:848-867 catches PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED at checkout and rethrows 409 STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_ORDERS «المتجر لا يستقبل طلبات جديدة حالياً — تواصل مع المتجر.» — the split this path lacks; docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:315 (ق-216) records the rule «المتسوّق لا يُسلَّم فاتورة التاجر». Reachability in a normal week: prisma/migrations/20260825150000_phase16_plans_entitlements/migration.sql:71,76 — free max_image_storage_mb=200, growth=2048; entitlement.service.ts:135,157-166 — every new merchant starts on growth for 30 days; entitlement.service.ts:132-133 claims «nothing a merchant uploads during the trial can strand him afterwards», which is false against the free plan he actually lands on (trial-expiry-scanner.ts:96-98). phase1-backend/tests/phase16.plans-entitlements.integration.ts:154-181 proves the split for checkout and the raw 403 for the merchant's product upload only; the shopper's prescription path has no test.

**العلاج المقترح:** In prescription.service.ts:51 wrap the call exactly as storefront.service.ts:860-867 does: catch PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED and throw a shopper-voiced 409 (e.g. STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_UPLOADS «تعذّر رفع الوصفة الآن — تواصل مع الصيدلية لإكمال طلبك.»), and add that code to the storefront-api.ts message map. Separately, enqueue a merchant notification (same outbox) when a shopper's upload is refused for his plan, so the merchant learns he is over his storage ceiling from the first refused customer rather than from a lost sale. Add a phase16 test asserting the prescription route never returns PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED.

### 16-20-24 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/product-catalog.service.ts:383`

**الادّعاء:** Product/variant edits write the merchant's absolute stockQuantity over a value read before the transaction and compute the inventory_movements delta from that stale read, so a sale that landed in between is silently undone and the trail no longer sums to the stock

**الأثر المزعوم:** The merchant running one stock pool across the till, WhatsApp and the storefront — the product's stated reason to exist (pos.service.ts:12-16). Reproduction with no race at all: the product editor snapshots `stockQuantity` when opened (client/src/components/ProductCatalogManager.tsx:230) and ALWAYS sends it on save even when only the description or price was touched (:231 `onSave({ …, stockQuantity: Number(quantity), … })`). Stock 10 on screen; a POS sale of 3 lands (pos.service.ts:376-385 → stock 7, movement −3); the merchant fixes a typo and saves → server sets stock back to 10 (:378-379/:381) and, because `requestedQuantity === defaultVariant.stockQuantity` from the stale read, writes NO movement row (:382 condition false). Stock is now 3 units above reality with a movement log that says 7. Downstream: the storefront and the till offer 3 phantom units; a checkout reserves them; at completion `settleStorefrontReservations` fails the `stockQuantity >= quantity` guard (storefront.service.ts:1238-1239, 422 INSUFFICIENT_AVAILABLE_STOCK) so a DELIVERED order cannot be marked completed and no ledger row is booked — the merchant is not paid until he «corrects» inventory with another absolute set, which the trail cannot explain either. With a stale mobile tab this is the everyday case, the same argument ق-219 accepted for the order board; the reservation-expiry job's own header names the outcome: «phantom stock … compounding forever and undetectable until a physical count» (reservation-expiry-job.ts:23-24).

**الدليل المزعوم:** updateProduct: :342 `existing = await scopedProduct(...)` (outside any transaction, includes variants) → :365-366 `data.stockQuantity = requestedQuantity` → :367 `prisma.$transaction` opens → :371 `defaultVariant = existing.variants.find(isDefault)` (the pre-transaction snapshot) → :378-379 `productVariant.update({ data: { stockQuantity: requestedQuantity } })` (absolute, no CAS) → :382-383 `if (requestedQuantity !== defaultVariant.stockQuantity)` then `delta: requestedQuantity - defaultVariant.stockQuantity` (stale). Same shape in updateVariant: :483 `scopedVariant` outside tx → :497-498 → :500-501 absolute update → :502-504 delta from `variant.stockQuantity` (stale). And adjustInventory → applyVariantInventory: :406-409 variant read outside → :393-396 absolute update + stale delta. Concurrent writers that decrement atomically in the same window: pos.service.ts:376-381 (`updateMany … decrement`), storefront.service.ts:1238 (completion), return-inspection.service.ts:186-189/205-208. HTTP schema accepts the field as optional (http/schemas/catalog.schema.ts:106,134) but the client sends it unconditionally (ProductCatalogManager.tsx:231). No test covers a concurrent adjust (grep of phase1-backend/tests for manual_adjustment/adjustInventory: only pg2/phase17/ledger-sign guards). Register: absent — D-48/PG-2 rows cover deletes only; nothing in docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md or PHASE18_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md names the absolute-set/stale-delta write.

**العلاج المقترح:** Make the stock write a compare-and-swap against the value the merchant saw: `updateMany({ where: { id, storeId, stockQuantity: <snapshot the client sends as expectedStockQuantity, or the value read inside the tx with FOR UPDATE> }, data: { stockQuantity: requested } })`, treat `count === 0` as 409 STOCK_CHANGED («تغيّر المخزون منذ فتح الشاشة — حدّث الشاشة وأعد المحاولة»), and compute `delta` from that same proven value; apply the same pattern at :394-396 and :501-504. On the client, send `stockQuantity` only when the field was actually edited (ProductCatalogManager.tsx:231).

### 16-20-25 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/product-catalog.service.ts:379`

**الادّعاء:** The product editor resends the stock it loaded on every save, and updateProduct writes that stale number over the default variant's live stock with no compare-and-swap, silently un-selling units sold since the page loaded and minting a fake «manual_adjustment» trail row.

**الأثر المزعوم:** One stock pool across the till, WhatsApp and the storefront is the premise of this market, and this path corrupts it from the most ordinary merchant action. A merchant opens his catalog (stock 10 on the default variant), his cashier rings 3 units on the till an hour later, then he opens «تعديل» on that product to fix a typo in the description and presses «حفظ». The editor was seeded with 10, sends stockQuantity: 10, and the server sets the variant back to 10 while writing +3 «manual_adjustment» to inventory_movements. The 3 sold units are now sellable again on the storefront (storefront.service.ts:935 availability = stock − reservations) and at the till (pos.service.ts:379); the next COD order is packed short or refused at the door, and the trail shows a restock nobody performed. The inverse direction is worse for money: if a return-inspection restock (+5, return-inspection.service.ts:186-192) or a RI restock landed in between, the overwrite erases it, the storefront refuses sales the merchant can fulfil, and a delivered COD order can fail completion at storefront.service.ts:1239 INSUFFICIENT_AVAILABLE_STOCK — the courier already collected the cash and the merchant cannot mark the order completed, so his wallet credit for it is stuck (the very failure pos.service.ts:344-352 warns about). As a side effect the catalog PATCH route (routes/catalog.ts:70, permission catalog.products.update) becomes a stock-writing endpoint, bypassing the inventory.update gate the dedicated route at :72 enforces.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Client: client/src/components/ProductCatalogManager.tsx:230 seeds `quantity` with `String(product.stockQuantity)` from the list state (the mirrored default-variant stock rendered at :203 as «الكمية»); :231 «حفظ» calls onSave with `stockQuantity: Number(quantity)` unconditionally; :169-172 saveProduct PATCHes `${base}/products/${product.id}` with that body. Route: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/catalog.ts:70 requires only catalog.products.update and calls updateProduct; schema phase1-backend/src/http/schemas/catalog.schema.ts:106 admits `stockQuantity` on update. Server: product-catalog.service.ts:342 `existing = scopedProduct(...)` is a plain findFirst (:59) OUTSIDE any transaction, no lock; :365-366 `requestedQuantity` from the body; :367 `prisma.$transaction` with default READ COMMITTED; :371 default variant taken from the stale `existing`; :378 `variantData.stockQuantity = requestedQuantity`; :379 `tx.productVariant.update({ where: { id }, data })` — an unconditional absolute overwrite, no `stockQuantity: existing.stockQuantity` predicate; :381 the same absolute value written to products; :382-384 movement `delta: requestedQuantity - defaultVariant.stockQuantity` (against the :342 read) with reason «manual_adjustment» plus enqueueLowStockIfCrossed. Writers whose committed decrements this erases: pos.service.ts:376-385 (guarded decrement + «pos_sale» movement), storefront.service.ts:1238-1240 (COD completion decrement + «storefront_cod_completed»), return-inspection.service.ts:186-192 (restock increment). Same shape on the variant PATCH: routes/catalog.ts:86 + catalog.schema.ts:134 + product-catalog.service.ts:497-505 (the shipped UI omits stock on PATCH at VariantManager.tsx:142, so only API callers hit it today); and applyVariantInventory :390-401 computes its delta at :396 from the :512 pre-transaction read, a narrower race of the same kind.

**العلاج المقترح:** Make stock a compare-and-swap inside the transaction: in updateProduct (and updateVariant) replace the unconditional update at :379/:501 with `tx.productVariant.updateMany({ where: { id, stockQuantity: defaultVariant.stockQuantity }, data })` and throw 409 STOCK_CHANGED («تغيّر المخزون منذ فتح الصفحة — حدّث ثم أعد المحاولة») when count !== 1, so the movement delta at :383/:504 is guaranteed true; and have ProductEditor.tsx:231 include `stockQuantity` only when the merchant actually changed it from the seeded value. Better still, drop stockQuantity from the catalog PATCH schemas (catalog.schema.ts:106, :134) so stock moves only through the inventory.update endpoints, where a reason is recorded.

### 16-20-26 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:864`

**الادّعاء:** The Free plan's 200-orders-a-month ceiling refuses every shopper at the last checkout step while the merchant is never told his shop stopped taking orders — and cancelled or refused-at-the-door COD orders count against the 200.

**الأثر المزعوم:** After the 30-day trial every merchant lands on Free (200 orders/month). The counter spends the quota on every non-POS order created this month regardless of outcome, so in a market where 20–40% of COD orders are refused or cancelled (the register's own D-05 number) the honest shop's 200 «orders» are ~120–160 deliveries — about five a day. From the 201st attempt, each shopper types his name, phone and full address and is then told «المتجر لا يستقبل طلبات جديدة حالياً — تواصل مع المتجر» with no contact link; the merchant receives no email, no WhatsApp, no home-screen banner, no digest line. His best month of the year is exactly when his shop goes silent, and he learns it from a customer's complaint. The code comment claims his screens «still read the true reason», but the only surface is a progress bar on the subscription page he has to open on purpose.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Free limit: prisma/migrations/20260825150000_phase16_plans_entitlements/migration.sql:70 (`('free','max_monthly_orders',200)`); every new merchant is trialled on growth then moved to free (phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:157-170; phase1-backend/src/notifications/trial-expiry-scanner.ts:59-97). Usage = `storeOrder.count({ createdAt >= monthStart, source != 'pos' })` with no status filter (entitlement.service.ts:79) — cancelled/rejected/returned rows all count. Gate: storefront.service.ts:846-867 catches PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED and throws 409 STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_ORDERS (:864); the guest door runs the same `checkout` (phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:88). Shopper side: client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:366 → client/src/pages/StorefrontCheckoutPage.tsx:245,257 as a page error after the form; that page has no contact/WhatsApp element (grep «whatsapp|تواصل» in the file: none). Merchant side: the outbox event types are order.created / order.status_changed / order.courier_dispatched (phase1-backend/src/notifications/notification-outbox.service.ts:53,98,132), merchant.daily_digest built only from ordersToday/revenueToday/waiting (phase1-backend/src/notifications/merchant-digest-scanner.ts:99-128), trial.ending (trial-expiry-scanner.ts:174), auth.password_reset — no plan event anywhere (grep «max_monthly|plan_limit|PLAN_LIMIT» in phase1-backend/src/notifications: none). The home payload carries no plan/usage field (phase1-backend/src/services/merchant-home.service.ts — the only `limit` is `limit: 5` for top products at :95). The one surface is client/src/components/SubscriptionPanel.tsx:118-130 (bar turns red at ratio ≥ 0.8) mounted only at /settings/subscription (client/src/pages/MerchantDashboard.tsx:224). The comment at storefront.service.ts:848-859 («What the merchant is told is his own screens' job, and every one of them still reads the true reason») is not backed by any screen that says the shop is refusing orders. docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:315 (ق-216): «السقف لم يتغيّر؛ تغيّر جمهور الجملة فقط». Not in the register.

**العلاج المقترح:** (1) Fire a merchant notification from the same place the refusal is thrown (or from a small scanner over `merchantUsage`): an outbox row `plan.orders_ceiling_reached` (and one at 80%) on the operational channel, and a red banner on MerchantHome when `used/limit ≥ 0.8` — `merchantEntitlementsView` already returns the numbers. (2) Exclude orders that ended `cancelled`/`rejected` from the count in entitlement.service.ts:79 (`status: { notIn: [...] }`) so a refused delivery does not spend the quota. (3) Expose the ceiling state in `public-config` and show the vacation-style notice with the store's WhatsApp link before the address form, not after it.

### 16-20-27 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:1239`

**الادّعاء:** A storefront order's stock reservation lapses silently 72 hours into «جديدة», nothing re-checks it when the merchant moves the order forward, and he learns only when pressing «مكتملة» — after the courier has collected the cash — through a sentence that names neither the product nor an action

**الأثر المزعوم:** The code's own comment (storefront.service.ts:1213-1216) says a Thursday-night order confirmed on Monday is normal fulfilment in Iraq; that is more than 72 hours, so this fires on ordinary weekend orders whenever the merchant has not clicked «قيد المعالجة» in time. Once released, the till's sellable slice and the storefront show the unit as available (only `status: "active"` is subtracted), so it sells at the counter. The merchant then confirms by phone, ships, the courier collects the cash, and «مكتملة» is refused with «المخزون المحجوز لم يعد كافياً لإكمال الطلب.» — «المحجوز» refers to a reservation he was never shown lapsing, no product or variant is named, and nothing says the remedy is an inventory correction. The order stays open, the commission entry (order-lifecycle.service.ts:287) and the shopper's loyalty points (:298) are never booked, and the merchant holds cash for an order the platform says he cannot fulfil.

**الدليل المزعوم:** TTL: phase1-backend/src/services/retention.ts:108 RESERVATION_TTL_HOURS = 72, DB default in prisma/migrations/20260904100000_d46_reservation_expiry/migration.sql:68. Release: phase1-backend/src/services/reservation-expiry-job.ts:93-108 selects `status: "active", expiresAt <= now, order: { status: "new" }`; :132-139 flips it to `released` / `expired`; :144-152 writes ONLY a delta-0 inventory movement — no order status event, no order note, no notification; the client has no label for `storefront_reservation_expired` or any reservation wording (grep of client/src for storefront_reservation_expired|انتهى الحجز|حجز in OrderWorkspace.tsx: zero matches). Nothing re-checks on the merchant's next step: phase1-backend/src/services/order-lifecycle.service.ts:15 allows only new → processing; :259 calls `settleStorefrontReservations`, which at storefront.service.ts:1178-1201 returns without touching reservations for any status other than cancelled/rejected/returned/completed. Availability everywhere counts only active reservations: storefront list :366-367, checkout guard :931-935 (`status: "active"`). At completion :1235 folds `released` + `releaseReason === "expired"` rows into `settleable`; :1238 decrements only where `stockQuantity >= quantity`; :1239 throws 422 INSUFFICIENT_AVAILABLE_STOCK "Reserved stock is no longer available for completion."; client/src/components/OrderWorkspace.tsx:52 renders it as «المخزون المحجوز لم يعد كافياً لإكمال الطلب.» with no line details and no next step.

**العلاج المقترح:** (1) In `settleStorefrontReservations` on `new → processing`, re-check `stockQuantity − SUM(active) ≥ quantity` for each `released/expired` row and either flip it back to `active` or refuse the transition with the same `lines` payload checkout already emits (:928), so the merchant learns before he ships. (2) Show the lapse on the order detail from the reservation rows: «انتهت مدة حجز المخزون لهذا الطلب بعد ٧٢ ساعة — تحقّق من توفر الكمية قبل التجهيز». (3) Make the completion refusal name the line and the action: «الكمية المطلوبة من «{product}» لم تعد في المخزون — صحّح مخزون المنتج من «الجرد» ثم أكمل الطلب».

### 16-20-28 · **عالية** · `phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:1238`

**الادّعاء:** Completing an order whose reservation lapsed decrements stock without subtracting other orders' active reservations, so it spends units a second shopper holds and that shopper's order can never complete

**الأثر المزعوم:** Stock 5. Shopper A orders 5 on Thursday night; nobody confirms over the weekend, so on Sunday the D-46 sweep releases A's hold. Shopper B orders the same 5 on Monday morning and passes the checkout guard. The merchant confirms A by phone, delivers it, marks it completed: A's lapsed reservation is decremented against `stockQuantity >= 5` alone, stock goes to 0, and B's active reservation now backs nothing. B's courier delivers and collects the cash; the merchant presses «مكتمل» and gets 422 INSUFFICIENT_AVAILABLE_STOCK — the whole transition rolls back, including recordOrderCompletion (the merchant's wallet credit for B) and awardLoyaltyPoints. Order B is stuck in out_for_delivery for good unless the merchant invents +5 stock with an adjustment, which corrupts his inventory instead. The code's own comment at lines 1230-1234 says this guard is «the honest answer» when units were «sold to someone else while the reservation was lapsed» — but it only sees sales that were already DECREMENTED (POS, completed orders); the storefront's own model, a RESERVATION, is invisible to it. And the window is not 72 h: `new → processing` never re-arms the hold, so an acknowledged order carries no claim on stock from the moment it expires until the day it completes — routinely several days under COD, by the file's own account (1213-1216).

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1. Checkout availability is stock minus SUM(active reservations): storefront.service.ts:931-940 (`groupBy … status: "active"`, `available = stockQuantity - reserved`); the reservation is written active at :1001. 2. The sweep selects active rows with expiresAt <= now whose order is still `new` (reservation-expiry-job.ts:93-108) and flips them to `released` / releaseReason `expired` with NO stock change (:132-139; header :21-25 says the units stay in stockQuantity). 3. Nothing re-arms the hold when the merchant acknowledges the order: `new → processing` is the only forward edge (order-lifecycle.service.ts:14-22); transitionOrderStatus calls settle at :259, and settleStorefrontReservations returns at storefront.service.ts:1201 for every status other than cancelled/rejected/returned/completed; the sweep excludes non-`new` orders (:101), so the released row simply persists. 4. A second checkout now sees `available = stock - 0` at :935 and inserts its own active reservation at :1001. 5. Completion of the first order: settleable includes `released` + `expired` rows (:1235); each is decremented with `productVariant.updateMany({ where: { id, storeId, stockQuantity: { gte: reservation.quantity } } })` — no term for other orders' active reservations (:1238) — then marked consumed (:1249). Contrast the till, which was fixed for exactly this pattern: pos.service.ts:362-367 sums active reservations and :379 guards `stockQuantity: { gte: quantity + reservedQty }`. 6. Completion of the second order: its `active` row hits the same guard at :1238 with stock already 0, `changed.count !== 1` → throw at :1239 → the transaction at order-lifecycle.service.ts:243 rolls back, taking recordOrderCompletion (:287) and awardLoyaltyPoints (:298) with it; the order stays out_for_delivery (:18 — only completed/returned lead out).

**العلاج المقترح:** Smallest: in settleStorefrontReservations, for rows with `status === "released"` (the expired path) compute `reservedByOthers = SUM(quantity) of active reservations on that variant with orderId != order.id` inside the same tx and guard with `stockQuantity: { gte: reservation.quantity + reservedByOthers }` — the till's rule at pos.service.ts:379 — so the lapsed order is the one refused, not the shopper who still holds a claim. Right moment: also handle `toStatus === "processing"` in settle — for each released/expired row check `stock - SUM(active, other orders) >= quantity` and flip it back to `active` (clear releaseReason/releasedAt), refusing the confirmation with INSUFFICIENT_AVAILABLE_STOCK naming the line otherwise, so the merchant learns during the confirmation call rather than at the door.

### 16-20-29 · متوسطة · `client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:161`

**الادّعاء:** The owner records a payment made outside the platform without the screen ever telling him how much to pay, and neither party's settlement list shows what was paid

The merchant's settlement rows print the same two fields (client/src/components/MerchantFinancePanel.tsx:14,124); only the LAST settlement's net is shown, on the wallet card (client/src/components/MerchantWalletCard.tsx:87). The net formula exists once, server-side (merchant-wallet.service.ts:80-82), and settlement rows already carry every component (finance.service.ts:338-353).

**العلاج المقترح:** Add `netPayoutIqd` (via the existing netPayout formula) to each merchant row of platformFinanceReport and to each row of merchantSettlements; print it as the headline of the settle confirm form («ستدفع للتاجر: X د.ع») and in the success notice, and add a «صافي المدفوع» figure to both settlement history rows.

### 16-20-31 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/auth/auth.service.ts:167`

**الادّعاء:** A rejected or suspended merchant who enters the correct password is told his credentials are wrong, and the password-reset door then swallows him silently

**الأثر المزعوم:** The honest shop owner whose application was rejected by mistake, or whose account was suspended over a dispute, reads «بيانات الدخول غير صحيحة. تحقق من البريد أو رقم الهاتف وكلمة المرور.» — a sentence that blames his typing for the platform's decision. He asks for a reset, is told it was queued, and nothing ever arrives; he tries again and is told his password is wrong again. Because the decision itself sends him nothing, the login door is the only place he can learn the outcome, and it lies to him. He ends up phoning whoever he can find, and the platform's own support cannot see why «reset» does nothing for him either.

**الدليل المزعوم:** loginMerchant verifies the password before any status check (auth.service.ts:154-157), so only a caller holding the real password reaches the rejected/!isActive branch, which answers 401 INVALID_MERCHANT_CREDENTIALS with the wrong-password sentence (auth.service.ts:166-168). The stated rationale is that a stolen account's holder must not learn the platform noticed (merchant-suspension.service.ts:25-28; auth.service.ts:162-165) — but the precedent one branch later tells the truth and gives an action: MERCHANT_NO_ACTIVE_STORE «حسابك سليم… تواصل مع إدارة المنصة» (auth.service.ts:176-177). Password reset resolves a rejected or inactive merchant to null (password-reset.service.ts:239-241) and then answers `delivery: "queued"` after constant-time busywork (password-reset.service.ts:446-448). The approval/rejection decision writes the row and an audit event only (registration.service.ts:135-147); no notification template for registration or approval exists under phase1-backend/src/notifications (grep for registration|approval|approved found none), so «ستصلك الموافقة قريباً» (auth.service.ts:160) is likewise a promise nothing keeps.

**العلاج المقترح:** At auth.service.ts:166 split the cases: `approvalStatus === "rejected"` → 403 MERCHANT_REGISTRATION_REJECTED «لم تُقبل طلب تسجيلك. للاستفسار تواصل مع إدارة المنصة.» and `!isActive` → 403 MERCHANT_SUSPENDED «حسابك موقوف حالياً. تواصل مع إدارة المنصة.», both carrying the published platform phone/WhatsApp from readPlatformContact so the sentence ends in an action, as MERCHANT_NO_ACTIVE_STORE already does. If the stolen-account concern must survive, apply the generic 401 only to suspensions whose recorded reason is flagged fraud, never to rejection.

### 16-20-32 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/auth/auth.service.ts:102`

**الادّعاء:** Owner 2FA is opt-in with no enforcement path, so the platform's root account can operate single-factor indefinitely — the more-privileged owner has weaker 2FA enforcement than his own staff

**الأثر المزعوم:** 19-أ's acceptance requires '2FA actually ON for the owner (not "ready to enable later")' and 'owner login without the second factor refused.' As shipped, the second factor is only challenged when the owner has already chosen to enrol; nothing ever forces enrolment. A sole owner who never opens the TOTP screen authenticates with a password alone, forever. This is the enabling precondition for finding 1 (password phish -> full session -> change email -> permanent lockout) and for owner-account compromise generally. Platform STAFF are held to a stricter rule than the owner: requirePlatformStaff refuses every non-enrolment route until totpEnabledAt is set (platform-permissions.ts:124-126), while the owner has no equivalent gate.

**الدليل المزعوم:** loginPlatformOwner challenges the code only inside if (owner.totpEnabledAt) (auth.service.ts:102-113); when it is null the function returns a full platform_owner session on the password alone (:122). The header comment at :81 confirms 'TOTP off — which is the default and the state of every account until an owner enrols.' The owner's request guard requirePlatformOwner (http/shared.ts:206-213) performs only verifyTypedSession + touchSession — no enrolment check — and every owner route (owner-security.ts:24,30,39,47,67,73; orders.ts:107 requireKind('platform_owner')) inherits that, so no code path forces the owner to enrol before reaching the dashboard. Compare platform-permissions.ts:124-126, which forces staff enrolment.

**العلاج المقترح:** Add an enrolment gate for the owner mirroring platform staff: after login, admit an unenrolled owner only to totpStatus/begin/confirm and refuse all other owner routes until totpEnabledAt is set (or block the ability to run in a deployed environment without owner TOTP). This makes 'owner login without the second factor refused' true in practice, not only once he has opted in.

### 16-20-33 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:129`

**الادّعاء:** Storefront login and registration are throttled per shared IP (12 and 8 per 10 minutes per store) with no refund on success — unlike the dashboard doors — so on a CGNAT mobile network the 13th shopper of a busy evening is told «كثرت المحاولات» for his first tap.

**الأثر المزعوم:** The code itself records that an Iraqi mobile network puts a whole neighbourhood behind one address and that a tight per-IP ceiling «would refuse real shoppers on a busy evening». The merchant and staff doors were given 30 attempts and a refund on every correct sign-in for exactly that reason; the customer doors — where the shared-IP problem is worst, during a live-sale rush — got 12 and 8, consumed on success too. The shopper who is refused sees a sentence that accuses him of trying too many times, gets no Retry-After, and if he falls back to guest checkout with a phone that already has an account he is bounced back to the same locked door for the rest of the window. The store loses the order at the peak minute, and the shopper blames the store.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Registration bucket storefront.ts:117 (`limitStorefrontWrite(request, "customer_register", request.ip, 8)`), login bucket :129 (`"customer_login"`, 12); key is `${storeSlug}:${ip}` with a 10-minute window (phase1-backend/src/http/shared.ts:94-96). `consumeStorefrontRateLimit` increments on every call and throws 429 RATE_LIMITED once the count exceeds the limit (phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:229-246); no Retry-After. Neither route refunds on success — the only refund callers are shared.ts:148-149 (checkout replay), phase1-backend/src/services/password.service.ts:91, owner-totp.service.ts:148 and phase1-backend/src/http/routes/auth.ts:54,84. Those dashboard doors use limit 30 and refund with the stated reason «on Iraqi mobile networks many merchants share one [address]. Without this refund, thirty honest sign-ins lock out the thirty-first» (auth.ts:45-54, 74-84). The same admission for shoppers: shared.ts:107-116 and docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:294 (ق-194), applied to cart writes and checkout (storefront.ts:90,183,205) but not to the two account doors. Shopper copy: client/src/pages/CustomerAuthPage.tsx:63 → client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:349 «كثرت المحاولات. انتظر قليلًا ثم حاول مجددًا.». The loop: phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:52 (409 for a phone with an account) → client/src/pages/StorefrontCheckoutPage.tsx:267 «سجّل الدخول بهذا الرقم لإتمام الطلب». Register: D-11 is marked closed with these per-IP keys as the fix (docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:120); the shared-IP cost of the customer doors has no row.

**العلاج المقترح:** After `loginCustomer`/`registerCustomer` succeed, refund the bucket exactly as auth.ts:54 does (one line each), raise `customer_login` to the dashboard's 30, and give registration the ق-194 two-bucket shape (a per-phone or per-guest-token bucket plus a wide address bucket) instead of address-only 8. Send `Retry-After` from storefront.service.ts:246. Reword RATE_LIMITED for the storefront so it does not accuse the person: «الشبكة مزدحمة من منطقتك الآن — حاول بعد دقائق قليلة». Add a register row for the shared-IP ceilings so D-11 stops reading as fully solved.

### 16-20-34 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:129`

**الادّعاء:** Storefront sign-in and register ceilings are keyed per store per IP with no refund on success, so twelve honest neighbours signing in behind one mobile IP lock out the thirteenth — the exact defect the merchant door was fixed for

**الأثر المزعوم:** Iraqi mobile carriers put a neighbourhood behind one CGNAT address (the code says so itself). With 8-hour customer sessions every returning shopper signs in again. On a promo evening a popular store gets more than 12 sign-ins (or 8 registrations) from one address in 10 minutes and the next shopper sees «كثرت المحاولات. انتظر قليلًا ثم حاول مجددًا.» — a message that blames him for the platform's rule, on a store he was about to pay at. If the store requires login to check out he cannot buy at all for 10 minutes.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:117 `limitStorefrontWrite(request, "customer_register", ip, 8)` and :129 `limitStorefrontWrite(request, "customer_login", ip, 12)` → phase1-backend/src/http/shared.ts:94-96 key `${storeSlug}:${ip}`, window 10 min; no refundStorefrontRateLimit anywhere in :114-137 (loginCustomer at phase1-backend/src/auth/auth.service.ts:286-300 has none either), so successful logins consume budget. The counter throws at phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:246. Contrast the merchant door: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/auth.ts:45-54 refunds on success, comment 'on Iraqi mobile networks many merchants share one [address] … thirty honest sign-ins lock out the thirty-first'; shared.ts:107-108 'Iraqi mobile networks put a whole neighbourhood behind one address'. Shopper message: client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:349. Session length that makes daily sign-in the norm: shared.ts:72, phase1-backend/src/config.ts:60.

**العلاج المقترح:** Mirror auth.ts:54 on both storefront doors: after loginCustomer/registerCustomer succeed, call `refundStorefrontRateLimit({ scope: "customer_login", rawKey: `${store.slug}:${request.ip ?? "unknown"}` })` (and the same for customer_register), so only failures spend budget; or switch them to limitStorefrontWriteWithAddressCeiling keyed on the normalized phone with a much higher per-address ceiling.

### 16-20-35 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:227`

**الادّعاء:** A guest who buys a prescription product has an 8-hour window to upload: the only upload route sits behind an account session, the guest has no password, the phone+order-number tracking page has no upload, and the pharmacist cannot upload for him — after that the order can never leave `new`.

**الأثر المزعوم:** The exact pharmacy case the feature exists for: a guest orders at 22:00 from his phone; the pharmacist opens the dashboard at 09:00 and rejects a blurry photo (rejection re-opens the upload by design). By then the checkout-issued session has expired, the shopper cannot sign in (no password, often no email for a reset), the tracking page cannot take a file, and the merchant's routes can only view or review. The gate keeps refusing new→processing, so the order sits until someone cancels it — a lost prescription sale per occurrence, and the same for any guest who simply uploads the next day.

**الدليل المزعوم:** guestCheckout → checkout (guest-checkout.service.ts:88); storefront.service.ts:992-993 stamps prescriptionStatus 'pending_upload' for guests exactly as for accounts. routes/storefront.ts:208-209 mints the guest's session at checkout; config.ts:60 sessionLifetimeSeconds = 60*60*8 → identity.ts:192 expiresAt in 8 h; the row has passwordHash: null (guest-checkout.service.ts:59) so loginCustomer cannot issue another, and the checkout form's email is «اختياري» (StorefrontCheckoutPage.tsx:279). The only upload route is POST /store/:slug/account/orders/:orderId/prescription (routes/storefront.ts:227) guarded by requireStorefrontCustomer (:40-43). The tracking page authenticates by order number + phone (StorefrontTrackOrderPage.tsx:38-44) but has no prescription section (client grep for 'prescription': six files, none is the tracking page). Merchant side: routes/orders.ts:152-162 exposes GET file and review only; reviewOrderPrescription refuses anything not pending_review (prescription.service.ts:121-123); rejection sets 'rejected' which re-opens the upload (:39). Gate: order-lifecycle.service.ts:253 blocks new→processing unless approved; transitions (:15) leave only cancelled/rejected. GuestPasswordCard.tsx:32-34 («اشتريت كضيف. اختر كلمة مرور…») is optional and only reachable while the session lives.

**العلاج المقترح:** Add the upload to the surface a guest can always reach: POST /store/:slug/track/:orderNumber/prescription that authenticates exactly as trackOrder does (order number + recipient phone) and calls uploadOrderPrescription with the order's own customerId; and add a merchant-side upload behind orders.update (the pharmacist holds the paper the courier collected) that writes pending_review through the same guarded updateMany at prescription.service.ts:61-65.

### 16-20-36 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/http/shared.ts:44`

**الادّعاء:** One shared `codiq_session` cookie for four dashboard identities, no `__Host-` prefix, and no revocation of the session a login or store switch replaces

**الأثر المزعوم:** This already hit the owner in production (ق-152): signing in as a merchant to test a store silently overwrote his owner cookie and he lost his dashboard while his owner session stayed live in the database. Every login or store switch leaves the replaced session valid for its remaining 8 hours with no cookie pointing at it — a token nobody on screen can see or end unless he signs back in under that identity — and the operational effect is permanent: whoever runs the platform and tests its stores cannot do both in one browser. The debt register files it as D-88 «needs the owner's decision», so the phase closed with this clause open.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Promise: task brief 19-أ «invalidation of the previous session on login/switch» and «per-identity cookies or invalidation of the old one with __Host-». Code: `setWebSession` (shared.ts:44-52) writes the single name `codiq_session`, path `/`, `secure: config.cookieSecure` (conditional, so the `__Host-` contract cannot hold), and `bearerToken` (shared.ts:15-21) plus a duplicate reader in phase1-backend/src/auth/staff-permissions.ts:100-106 read that one name for owner, merchant, staff and platform_staff alike. The four dashboard login routes call `setWebSession` without reading or revoking the session already in the cookie (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/auth.ts:26, :55, :85, :97); the merchant store switch mints a new session with `createTypedSession` and leaves the old one live (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/merchant.ts:43-48, :61). Nothing calls `endCurrentSession` outside `/auth/logout` (sessions.ts:24-33). Acknowledged: docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:200 (D-88, «وقع فعلاً على الإنتاج … يحتاج قرار المالك أولاً») and docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:249 (ق-152 «لم تُغيَّر تسمية الكعكة»).

**العلاج المقترح:** Issue per-identity cookies named `__Host-codiq_session_<kind>` (secure always, path `/`, no domain — the customer cookie already shows the per-scope pattern at shared.ts:71-73), read them by the identity the route expects so two identities can coexist in one browser (which also answers D-88's logout question), and on every login and on `/merchant/stores/switch` read the cookie being replaced and revoke that session row before writing the new one.

**الادّعاء:** Abandoned-cart recovery ships no «التقاط مبكر» at all and can only write to registered customers with an email, so in a phone-first market the scanner has almost nobody to message.

**الأثر المزعوم:** The merchant switches the tool on, picks a recovery coupon, and the list fills with «ضيف — بلا وسيلة تواصل» and «بلا بريد — لا تُراسَل»: a browsing shopper never leaves a phone before submit, and the ق-201 guest who bought once with a phone and no email is excluded on his next abandoned cart. The platform's own ledger says email is secondary here (ق-131) and already ships a manual WhatsApp channel (ق-139) that this feature does not use. D-24's promised recovery is met by a scanner that, for the population this platform serves, sends nothing.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Promise: CODIQ_FULL_PLAN_26_PHASES.md:224 «التقاط مبكر + تسلسل رسائل فوق Outbox»; ledger ق-37 (DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:84) narrows to «التقاط مبكر + رسالة» but keeps early capture. Code: candidates require ownerKind 'customer', customerId not null and customer.email not null (abandoned-cart-scanner.ts:96-100); channel is the literal 'email' (:154); one message per cart forever (:17-27, dedupeKey :156, stamp :168). No capture exists before submit: a browsing cart is ownerKind 'guest' with only a token hash (storefront.service.ts:264-268); the first time a phone is stored is guestCustomerFor at submit (routes/storefront.ts:207; guest-checkout.service.ts:47-59), i.e. only for carts that convert; a guest's later carts are customer-owned but fail :100 when he left the «اختياري» email blank (StorefrontCheckoutPage.tsx:279). A client grep for 'abandoned' hits only the merchant panel and navigation — no shopper-side capture component. The panel admits it: AbandonedCartsPanel.tsx:123 «تصل الزبائن المسجلين بعناوين بريد فقط. الضيوف لا وسيلة تواصل معهم فلا يُراسَلون أبداً», :185 «ضيف — بلا وسيلة تواصل», :186 «بلا بريد — لا تُراسَل». Ledger ق-131 (DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:228): «في العراق واتساب هو قناة الإشعار والبريد ثانوي»; ق-139 (:236) ships wa.me links for orders only.

**العلاج المقترح:** Implement the promised early capture: when the guest checkout phone field is completed (blur), call a small POST /store/:slug/checkout/identify that runs guestCustomerFor and mergeGuestCartIntoCustomer (both already exported, guest-checkout.service.ts:34) so the cart becomes customer-owned with a phone before submit. Then let the scanner select on customer.phoneNormalized (not email) and surface each abandoned row as a ق-139-style «أرسل عبر واتساب» wa.me link with the prepared Arabic message and coupon — outside the outbox CHECK, as ق-139 did — keeping the email outbox row for the few who gave one.

### 16-20-38 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/notifications/abandoned-cart-scanner.ts:100`

**الادّعاء:** Abandoned-cart recovery selects and messages only customers with an email, while the storefront registers shoppers by phone with email optional — so the feature reaches almost no Iraqi shopper and gives the merchant no way to act by hand.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant switches the feature on, attaches a recovery coupon, and watches named customers' carts with money in them sit under «بلا بريد — لا تُراسَل» and «إحصائية فقط»; nothing is sent and the panel does not even show the phone number he registered them with, so he cannot pick up WhatsApp and recover the sale himself. The tool is inert in this market by construction, not by a shopper's choice.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Registration: auth.service.ts:257-258 calls normalizePhone unconditionally (identity.ts:65-72 throws INVALID_PHONE without one) and normalizeOptionalEmail (identity.ts:33-36 → null); storefront.schema.ts:67 «`email` is genuinely optional». Scanner: abandoned-cart-scanner.ts:96-100 candidate filter `ownerKind: "customer", customerId not null, customer: { email: { not: null } }`; :154-155 channel "email", recipient = email. Panel service: abandoned-cart.service.ts:135 selects only fullName and email for the customer — phone never reaches the panel; :155 `hasEmail`. Panel UI: client/src/components/AbandonedCartsPanel.tsx:123 «…تصل الزبائن المسجلين بعناوين بريد فقط…», :186 badge «بلا بريد — لا تُراسَل», :190 «إحصائية فقط» — no phone, no link, no per-row action. The worker already has an SMS send path that no producer uses: outbox-worker.ts:446-447 `provider.sendSms`.

**العلاج المقترح:** Smallest change: in abandonedCartsPanel select `customer.phone`/`phoneNormalized` for customer-owned carts and render a per-row «واتساب» (wa.me/<E.164 without +>?text=…) and «اتصال» (tel:) action in AbandonedCartsPanel.tsx so the merchant can recover the cart on the channel the shopper actually uses. Follow-up: let the scanner enqueue channel "sms" to phoneNormalized when the customer has no email and the store has an SMS sender, keeping the same dedupe key and marketing cap.

### 16-20-39 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/services/analytics.service.ts:157`

**الادّعاء:** salesSeries and topProducts sum total_iqd for every order that is not cancelled/rejected — so door-refused «returned» orders and unconfirmed «new» orders count as sales — while the revenue card on the same screen is the ledger net of returns; the two figures disagree by the store's refusal volume.

**الأثر المزعوم:** In this market a refused delivery at the door is the normal return (order-lifecycle.service.ts:18 out_for_delivery → returned), and no money changed hands. A pharmacy with 25% refusals sees «الإيراد المحقق» (ledger, returns reversed) roughly a third below the sales curve drawn directly above it, and the «أفضل المنتجات» table ranks its most-refused product as the top seller by both units and revenue. The merchant restocks and promotes the product that keeps coming back, and cannot reconcile the two numbers the dashboard shows him for the same period. The home screen's «المبيعات» number (merchant-home.service.ts:56) carries the same over-count.

**الدليل المزعوم:** analytics.service.ts:27 defines NON_CONSUMING_STATUSES = ["cancelled","rejected"], copied from the COUPON consumption rule in coupon.service.ts:64-67 («Returned orders DID ship — those keep consuming»). :150-163 salesSeries runs `SUM(total_iqd)` over `status NOT IN (cancelled, rejected)` (:157) — returned, new, processing, preparation, out_for_delivery all count at full order value — while its own comment at :158-159 says the curve «must draw the same money» as the ledger cards. :181-200 topProducts sums `line_total_iqd` under the identical predicate (:192). :62-110 analyticsSummary instead aggregates ledger_entries where :98 makes order_returned subtract and :110 derives revenueIqd; order-lifecycle.service.ts:302-303 writes that reversal on the returned edge. The client renders both on one screen: client/src/components/MerchantAnalytics.tsx:203 «الإيراد المحقق» from summary.revenue.revenueIqd, :227 the series chart, :249 per-product revenueIqd. merchant-home.service.ts:26-27 builds REAL_ORDERS from the same NON_CONSUMING_STATUSES and :56 sums totalIqd over it.

**العلاج المقترح:** Keep NON_CONSUMING_STATUSES for coupon usage only and introduce a sales-specific set for the aggregates: at analytics.service.ts:157, :192 and merchant-home.service.ts:26 exclude `returned` as well (`status NOT IN ('cancelled','rejected','returned')`), or restrict the sales curve and best-sellers to `completed` so they draw exactly the money the ledger books; if in-flight orders should still be visible, expose them as a separate «طلبات قيد التنفيذ» series rather than folding them into «المبيعات».

### 16-20-40 · متوسطة · `phase1-backend/src/services/coupon.service.ts:67`

**الادّعاء:** A cash-on-delivery parcel refused at the door is counted as a consumed coupon use, so the shopper who re-orders is told he has exhausted the coupon and the total-use budget burns on non-sales.

**الأثر المزعوم:** With refusal-at-door the normal Iraqi return, a «first order» coupon (maxUsesPerCustomer 1) is spent by a delivery that never happened; the shopper's next attempt gets «استخدمت هذا الكوبون بالحد الأقصى المسموح لحسابك.» and blames the shop. A capped campaign (maxUsesTotal 100) loses roughly its refusal rate of the budget to orders the platform's own ledger records as no sale, and the marketing report shows those as «الاستخدامات المحتسبة».

**الدليل المزعوم:** coupon.service.ts:64-67 consumingOrder = status notIn ["cancelled","rejected"] — `returned` consumes; :68-70 total cap → COUPON_EXHAUSTED; :72-74 per-customer → COUPON_CUSTOMER_LIMIT_REACHED; both run under the checkout lock at :112 and in the preview at :143. The merchant has no non-consuming status to choose: order-lifecycle.service.ts:18 `out_for_delivery: ["completed", "returned"]` — a failed COD delivery can only become `returned`. The ledger treats the same order as never sold: finance.service.ts:72-76 recordOrderReturn returns "no_completion_entry" when there is no order_completed entry, i.e. nothing was booked for a door refusal. Shopper copy: client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:356. Report side: analytics.service.ts:258-262 couponsUsage counts it as consuming; client/src/components/MerchantAnalytics.tsx:282 states the exclusion is only «الملغاة والمرفوضة».

**العلاج المقترح:** Treat a return that never completed as non-consuming, at the single definition in coupon.service.ts (and mirror it in analytics.service.ts couponsUsage): `order: { OR: [{ status: { notIn: ["cancelled","rejected","returned"] } }, { status: "returned", statusEvents: { some: { toStatus: "completed" } } }] }` — a return after completion still consumes, a refusal at the door does not.
