### 16-20-59 · منخفضة · `client/src/pages/MerchantDashboard.tsx:101`

**الادّعاء:** Merchant dashboard page headers still tell the merchant that Products, Marketing, Stores and Settings are «structure only / experimental / not wired», and every non-product page wears a «وضع التاجر التجريبي» pill — above fully working screens; the register's D-83 names only the Settings group.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant opening «متى ينفد مخزونك» or «المراجعات» reads «مساحة تجريبية لهيكل المنتج والمخزون، جاهزة للربط في مرحلة لاحقة فقط» above his real stock forecast; opening «الكوبونات» reads «دون حملات أو قسائم … فعلية» above his live coupon engine; opening «جميع المتاجر» reads «لا يعرض حاليًا أي متجر»; the header pill on production says he is in a demo mode. For a shop owner deciding whether to trust his money and catalogue to the platform this is the platform calling itself a mock-up, and it violates the project's own ق-10 honesty rule. Cost is trust and support calls rather than money — hence low — but it is the sentence he reads most often. It also shows the register is drifting in both directions: this row is under-scoped, while other rows are stale closed or stale open (see evidence).

**الدليل المزعوم:** Groups whose every entry is `status: "implemented"`: client/src/config/navigation.ts:200-215 (المنتجات, 7 entries), :239-249 (التسويق), :250-275 (الإعدادات, 11 entries), :280-287 (المتاجر). `getPagePresentation` (MerchantDashboard.tsx:83-110) returns: :99 المتاجر «هيكل هذه المساحة مخصص لتاجر واحد ومتاجره؛ لا يعرض حاليًا أي متجر أو بيانات تشغيلية.», :101 المنتجات «مساحة تجريبية لهيكل المنتج والمخزون، جاهزة للربط في مرحلة لاحقة فقط.», :103 التسويق «هيكل للعروض والحملات والتواصل دون حملات أو قسائم أو عمليات إرسال فعلية.», :105 الإعدادات «تظهر وجهات الإعدادات كبنية فقط؛ لا توجد تغييرات حفظ أو تكاملات أو إعدادات دفع في هذه المرحلة.». The text is rendered as the page-header paragraph `{pageMeta.detail}` at :223 for every route outside `productMode` (:200), i.e. above StockForecastPanel, ReviewModeration, CouponsManager, StoresManager, DeliveryRatesManager, NotificationCenter, MarketingIntegrationsPanel, PosDevicesPanel mounted at :224; the header pill « وضع التاجر التجريبي» is at :222 on the same branch. Register: docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:196 (D-83) names الإعدادات only; D-55/FE-8 was closed on navigation statuses (register :126) while this copy stayed. Register sample verdicts from this audit (≥8 rows, all severities): D-10 High closed ✔ (phase1-backend/src/http/shared.ts:222-226); D-15 High closed ✔ (phase1-backend/src/services/finance.service.ts:236 groupBy); D-16 High «half-open» honest ✔ (finance.service.ts:333-336 `findMany` with no `take` in createSettlement); D-46 Medium closed ✔ (phase1-backend/src/services/reservation-expiry-job.ts:161-194; retention.ts:108); D-39 Medium closed ✔ (phase1-backend/src/services/retention-job.ts:90-178); D-78 Medium listed as «مكسور اليوم» (register :180) but FIXED — phase1-backend/src/http/errors.ts:234-238 + phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/fk-violations.ts:60-118 answer cart_items/inventory_reservations with Arabic 409s; D-53 Medium listed wholly open (register :168) but half fixed — maximum-scale removed (client/index.html:6-9), `window.confirm` still at client/src/components/VariantManager.tsx:156, StorefrontSettingsPanel.tsx:185, ProductCatalogManager.tsx:187; D-66 Low listed open (register :172) but half fixed — unknown /api/* is JSON 404 (phase1-backend/src/http/app.ts:180-194) while /health still prints NODE_ENV (app.ts:60); D-18 High listed open (register :167) but the two named platform lists are keyset-paginated (phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:381-418, 485-513); D-64 Low open ✔ (phase1-backend/src/http/routes/merchant.ts:67-69, `customers.view` on POST); D-81 Medium open ✔ (phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/soft-delete.service.ts:315, 476-486 — `restoreCategory` takes no override); D-88 High open ✔ (shared.ts:45-51, one `codiq_session` cookie); D-69 Low open ✔ (shared.ts:50 literal 8h); D-12 High stale (finding 1).

**العلاج المقترح:** In `getPagePresentation`, drop the placeholder sentences for any group whose entries are all `implemented` (derive it from `merchantNavGroups` rather than hand-writing per group) and give those pages a one-line factual eyebrow or none; replace the «وضع التاجر التجريبي» pill at :222 with the store name/plan (keep it only under `config.demoMode`). Widen D-83 in the register to the four groups and the pill, and update D-78, D-53, D-66 and D-18 with the file:line evidence above so the phase-26 gate is measured against the code, not the 2026-08-25 snapshot.

### 16-20-60 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:107`

**الادّعاء:** The resolver's claim that «the check and the write see the same snapshot» is not true: every gate is a READ COMMITTED count-then-insert with no lock, and five of them run outside any transaction, so concurrent requests at the ceiling all pass

**الأثر المزعوم:** Low cost per incident: a merchant at 99/100 products who double-submits lands 101, or a scripted merchant fires N parallel creates and lands N. It matters to the platform's tiers, not to a shopper, and it needs either concurrency or intent. Reported because the acceptance criterion is about direct API calls at the ceiling and the comment in the resolver promises an atomicity the code does not have.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1. The claim: phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:105-109 («Runs inside the caller's transaction when one is provided so the check and the write see the same snapshot»); the check is a plain count: :110-114. 2. No isolation level is set globally (grep isolationLevel|transactionOptions in phase1-backend/src/db/prisma.ts → no matches) — Postgres default READ COMMITTED; only checkout and POS opt into Serializable: storefront.service.ts:561, :1008; pos.service.ts:431. Under READ COMMITTED two transactions each count N-1, each insert, both commit; nothing is locked (no FOR UPDATE on the merchant row anywhere in entitlement.service.ts). 3. Gates that run inside a transaction but unlocked: product create product-catalog.service.ts:326-334; duplicate :581-596; store create merchant-store.service.ts:119-126; CSV import csv-transfer.service.ts:301 → :319. 4. Gates that run with the bare client, then write in a separate statement or transaction: staff.service.ts:91 (prisma) → :97 / :108-110; product-catalog.service.ts:630 (prisma) → :636 loop; store-banner.service.ts:143 → :148; storefront-config.service.ts:259; prescription.service.ts:51.

**العلاج المقترح:** Serialize the check per merchant instead of per request: at the top of assertWithinPlanLimit, when the client is a transaction, take `SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(merchantId))` (or `SELECT id FROM merchants WHERE id = $1 FOR UPDATE`) before counting; and move the five bare-client call sites (staff.service.ts:91, product-catalog.service.ts:630, store-banner.service.ts:143, storefront-config.service.ts:259, prescription.service.ts:51) inside the transaction that performs their write so the lock covers the insert. Correct the comment at entitlement.service.ts:107-108 to describe the lock rather than a snapshot.

### 16-20-61 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:114`

**الادّعاء:** assertWithinPlanLimit is a bare count-then-insert with no lock, run under READ COMMITTED (or outside any transaction), so N parallel requests one below the ceiling all pass and every limit except monthly orders can be overshot at will.

**الأثر المزعوم:** The platform owner, and only against a merchant who scripts it: a free-plan merchant at 2 of 3 stores who fires twenty `POST /merchant/stores` at once gets up to twenty stores; the same for products (100), CSV imports, staff seats and image bytes. Each overshoot is bounded by how many requests he fires, but nothing stops him repeating it, so the plan ceilings for stores, products, staff and storage are advisory against anyone who wants past them. The one limit that matters most to the platform's money — max_monthly_orders — is NOT affected because checkout runs Serializable.

**الدليل المزعوم:** The resolver reads a count and compares: phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:110-121 (`usage = await merchantUsage(...)`; `if (usage[feature] + increment > limit) throw`), and `merchantUsage` at :66-103 is plain `count`/`aggregate` calls — no `FOR UPDATE`, no advisory lock. Callers that do wrap it in a transaction never raise the isolation level: `createMerchantStore` phase1-backend/src/services/merchant-store.service.ts:119 (`prisma.$transaction(async (transaction) => …)` with no options), check at :121, insert at :126 → phase1-backend/src/services/store-bootstrap.service.ts:28; `createProduct` phase1-backend/src/services/product-catalog.service.ts:326 (no options), check :330, insert :334; `duplicateProduct` :581/:584/:596; CSV import phase1-backend/src/services/csv-transfer.service.ts:301. Two callers check with the ROOT client outside the transaction that inserts: `createStoreStaff` phase1-backend/src/services/staff.service.ts:91 (`assertWithinPlanLimit(prisma, …)`) then inserts at :97 / :108-110; `addProductImages` phase1-backend/src/services/product-catalog.service.ts:630 (`assertWithinPlanLimit(prisma, …)`) then inserts at :636 (likewise store-banner.service.ts:143, storefront-config.service.ts:259, prescription.service.ts:51). The only `isolationLevel: Serializable` in the services tree are pos.service.ts:431, storefront.service.ts:561 and storefront.service.ts:1008 (the checkout that guards max_monthly_orders at :861); no migration under prisma/migrations sets `default_transaction_isolation`, so every other gated write runs at Postgres's READ COMMITTED default, where twenty concurrent transactions each observe count = limit−1 and each commits its own insert.

**العلاج المقترح:** Make the check-and-write atomic per merchant with the smallest possible change: at the top of `assertWithinPlanLimit` (entitlement.service.ts:110) take a transaction-scoped advisory lock keyed on the merchant — `await client.$executeRaw\`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(${merchantId}))\`` — and move the five root-client callers (staff.service.ts:91, product-catalog.service.ts:630, store-banner.service.ts:143, storefront-config.service.ts:259, prescription.service.ts:51) so the check and their insert share one `prisma.$transaction`. Alternatively run those transactions with `isolationLevel: Serializable` inside `withSerializableRetry` (phase1-backend/src/db/serializable-retry.ts:64), as checkout already does.

### 16-20-62 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:58`

**الادّعاء:** When a trial's `ends_at` lapses, `activePlanForMerchant` refuses rather than degrading to the default plan, so for up to one scanner interval every checkout on the merchant's storefront returns a 403 carrying the merchant-facing message «لا يوجد اشتراك نشط لهذا التاجر؛ راجِع إدارة المنصة.» and every «تم التسليم» completion rolls back.

**الأثر المزعوم:** Every trial merchant hits this window exactly once, on day 31, for up to 10 minutes (`TRIAL_EXPIRY_SCAN_INTERVAL_MS`, env-overridable) — and for as long as the standalone worker is down, since only worker-entry.ts starts the scanner. During the window a shopper tapping «تأكيد الطلب» is shown a 403 with a sentence written for the merchant and telling him to contact platform management; a COD shopper refused at the last tap has no card on file to bring him back. The merchant marking a delivered order as completed gets the same 403 and the ledger entry is not booked (correctly rolled back, but he must retry later without being told why). ق-216 closed exactly this leak for PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED and left NO_ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION open.

**الدليل المزعوم:** entitlement.service.ts:53-58: `findFirst` requires `status: "active"` AND (`endsAt: null` OR `endsAt > now`); a lapsed trial row matches nothing and :58 throws `HttpError(403, "NO_ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION", …)`. trial-expiry-scanner.ts:45 sets the interval to 10 minutes; worker-entry.ts:55 is the only `startTrialExpiryScanner` call. storefront.service.ts:861 calls `assertWithinPlanLimit` → :111 `activePlanForMerchant`; the catch at :862-866 converts only `error.code === "PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED"` to the shopper-safe 409, so the 403 propagates to the shopper. order-lifecycle.service.ts:243 opens the transition transaction, :287 calls `recordOrderCompletion`, finance.service.ts:52 calls `activePlanForMerchant(tx, …)` → the same throw aborts the whole transition.

**العلاج المقترح:** In `activePlanForMerchant` (entitlement.service.ts:53-58), when no live row is found, look for the merchant's most recent `status: "active"` row with a lapsed `endsAt` and resolve it to the `DEFAULT_PLAN_CODE` plan's summary (read-side degrade; the scanner remains the writer). Independently, widen the catch at storefront.service.ts:863 to also translate `NO_ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION` into `STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_ORDERS` so no merchant-facing sentence ever reaches a shopper.

### 16-20-63 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:45`

**الادّعاء:** A plan created through `POST /platform/plans` with a partial or absent `limits` object stores only the given features, and `planSummary` treats every missing feature as a limit of 0 — assigning such a plan silently turns the merchant's storefront checkout off.

**الأثر المزعوم:** An owner who creates, say, an «enterprise» plan with `{"limits": {"max_products": 50000}}` via the API (or any future client that sends only the fields it shows) and moves a paying merchant onto it gives that merchant `max_monthly_orders = 0`: every shopper is refused with «المتجر لا يستقبل طلبات جديدة حالياً — تواصل مع المتجر.» on the first order of the month, and the merchant's own panel reads «طلبات الشهر 0 / 0». Nothing refuses the assignment and nothing in the database requires a plan to carry all five features. The current owner UI happens to send all five (PlansManager.tsx:171,191), so today this is reachable only by direct API call — which the phase's acceptance criterion explicitly covers.

**الدليل المزعوم:** platform.schema.ts:47 defines `planLimitsSchema` as `z.partialRecord(z.enum(PLAN_FEATURE_CODES), …)` and :93 makes it `.optional()` on `createPlanSchema`. entitlement.service.ts:313 `const limitEntries = body.limits === undefined ? [] : validatedLimitEntries(body.limits)` and :326 `createMany` inserts only those entries. Migration 20260825150000 lines 20-36 constrain `feature_code` to the five names and `limit_value >= 0` but nothing requires all five per plan. entitlement.service.ts:45 seeds `limits` with 0 for every code and :46-48 overwrites only the codes present. `assignMerchantSubscription` :436-437 accepts any `isActive` plan; `decideUpgradeRequest` :532-536 likewise. storefront.service.ts:861 → entitlement.service.ts:114 `usage + 1 > 0` is true for every order → PLAN_LIMIT_EXCEEDED → storefront.service.ts:864 `STORE_NOT_ACCEPTING_ORDERS`.

**العلاج المقترح:** For creation, replace `planLimitsSchema.optional()` at platform.schema.ts:93 with a full `z.record(z.enum(PLAN_FEATURE_CODES), planLimitValueSchema)` (keep `partialRecord` for PATCH, which cannot remove a feature). Belt-and-braces in `createPlan` (entitlement.service.ts:313): refuse with 422 when `limitEntries.length !== PLAN_FEATURE_CODES.length`. Optionally also refuse `assignMerchantSubscription`/`decideUpgradeRequest` for any plan whose `features.length < 5`.

### 16-20-64 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/entitlement.service.ts:538`

**الادّعاء:** `decideUpgradeRequest` checks `status === "pending"` outside the transaction and then updates the request with no status predicate, so two overlapping decisions both act — approve+reject leaves the request `rejected` while the subscription was already moved.

**الأثر المزعوم:** Requires two decisions in flight within the same few milliseconds — an owner double-clicking «موافقة», or the owner and a `plans.manage` staff member (19-ب) working the same queue. Double-approve: the second pass re-cancels the just-created subscription and creates another (resetting `startedAt`) and writes a second `upgrade_request_decided` audit row, or fails with P2002 on `merchant_subscriptions_one_active` and shows the owner an error after a success. Approve-then-reject: the merchant is on the requested plan while the row says «مرفوض» with the rejecter's name — no reversal, and the audit trail contradicts the subscription table.

**الدليل المزعوم:** entitlement.service.ts:522-525 reads the request with `prisma` (outside any transaction); :527 refuses when `status !== "pending"`; :528 opens `prisma.$transaction` with no `isolationLevel` (default READ COMMITTED — contrast storefront.service.ts:1008 which passes `Serializable`); :532-535 re-checks only the PLAN's `isActive`; :536 `replaceActiveSubscription` cancels/creates subscription rows; :538-547 `tx.planUpgradeRequest.update({ where: { id: requestId }, data: { status, decidedAt, decidedBy… } })` — no `status: "pending"` in the predicate and no row lock. Migration 20260826180000 lines 27-28: the unique index covers only `status = 'pending'`, so nothing structural stops a second decision from overwriting the first.

**العلاج المقترح:** Inside the transaction, before `replaceActiveSubscription`, do `const claimed = await tx.planUpgradeRequest.updateMany({ where: { id: requestId, status: "pending" }, data: { status: action === "approve" ? "approved" : "rejected", decidedAt: new Date(), …actor columns } }); if (claimed.count !== 1) throw new HttpError(409, "ALREADY_DECIDED", "هذا الطلب محسوم بالفعل.");` then perform the subscription write and audit, and re-read the row for the response. The same shape (read outside, unguarded update inside) exists in `decideMerchantApproval` at registration.service.ts:131-136.

### 16-20-65 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/finance.service.ts:330`

**الادّعاء:** An earlier, non-overlapping period can never be settled once any later period has been settled for the same merchant — a permanent, immutable gap the register does not carry.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A platform operator who settles March before February for a COD merchant (or types one wrong end date) leaves that merchant's February ledger entries unsettleable inside the system forever: the guard refuses, the row cannot be corrected (settlements are append-only), and the merchant's wallet window ("the next window opens where the settled one closed") silently skips the span — so his commission reconciliation for that month can only happen off-book. Nothing in the register (D-51, D-93, D-94) names this; today's cost is nil because production has no settlements yet, which is exactly why it belongs in the register before the first one.

**الدليل المزعوم:** finance.service.ts:325-329 loads `previous` as the merchant's settlement with the greatest `periodTo`; :330-331 throws 409 `SETTLEMENT_PERIOD_OVERLAP` whenever `from < previous.periodTo`, and the Arabic message tells the operator to start "من ذلك التاريخ أو بعده" — the comment at :320-324 calls the rule CONTIGUITY, not overlap. The database constraint that backs it (prisma/migrations/20260904142000_rt_money_settlement_no_overlap/migration.sql:46-47) is an `&&` overlap exclusion and would accept a disjoint earlier range, so the service guard is strictly tighter than the constraint. Immutability: the audit report docs/history/reports/PHASE18_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md:62 cites the append-only trigger on `settlements` (migrations/20260825190000…:80-90) and createSettlement's own comment at :318 says a wrong row is "permanent". The wallet consequence is asserted by phase1-backend/tests/phase17.finance.integration.ts:206. The suite tests a repeat (:220-222) and a wider swallowing range (:224-225) but never a disjoint earlier range. Register: line 77 (D-51), lines 335-358 (D-93, D-94) — none mentions the gap.

**العلاج المقترح:** Register it now (Low, S, before the first real settlement, beside D-93/D-94). When fixed: replace the `from < previous.periodTo` refusal with a true overlap test across all of the merchant's settlements (`NOT EXISTS … tstzrange(period_from, period_to) && tstzrange(from, to)`), so the guard and the exclusion constraint agree, and surface a non-blocking warning when a period is non-contiguous with the last one.

### 16-20-66 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/finance.service.ts:105`

**الادّعاء:** createManualReturnAdjustment checks for a prior reversal and then inserts under READ COMMITTED with no unique index on ledger_entries(order_id, entry_type), so two finance.settle actors submitting the same order concurrently both book a manual_adjustment on the immutable ledger and the merchant is docked twice

**الأثر المزعوم:** The merchant, permanently. Every reader treats each non-`order_completed` entry as a subtraction (`totalsFromGroups` :192-202, `totalsFromEntries` :178-189, wallet `signedTotals` merchant-wallet.service.ts:52-62, the analytics copy), so a duplicate manual_adjustment removes `subtotal + delivery − commission` from the payout a second time for one return; `ledger_entries_immutable` (20260825190000_phase17…/migration.sql:57-59) forbids deleting it and the only compensating instrument the platform has writes more negative rows, so there is no in-app correction. Likelihood is low: the single-user double click is blocked by the `busy` flag (FinanceCenter.tsx:189), so it needs the owner and a `finance.settle` staff member (platform.ts:219 — the accountant role of 19-ب) acting on the same late return within one round-trip, or a retried POST. The register's D-51 named exactly this class for settlements and got a constraint; the adjustment writer next to it did not.

**الدليل المزعوم:** platform.ts:217-221 → `createManualReturnAdjustment`. finance.service.ts:102 `prisma.$transaction(async (tx) …)` with no isolation option (Postgres default READ COMMITTED; db/prisma.ts:7 sets none). :103 reads the completion entry; :105 `reversed = findFirst({ orderId, entryType in ['order_returned','manual_adjustment'] })`; :106 throws only if found; :107-108 checks the order is `returned`; :109-122 `ledgerEntry.create(... entryType: 'manual_adjustment')`. Two transactions interleaving :105 before either reaches :109 both see null and both insert. Constraints on the table: phase17 migration.sql:29-47 — CHECKs on entry_type/bps and three non-unique indexes; the only later migration touching the table (20260904140000_rt_money_ledger_loyalty/migration.sql:29-39) adds a nullable column and a CHECK; no unique or exclusion constraint on (order_id, entry_type) anywhere in prisma/migrations. The sibling writer `recordOrderReturn` (:71-97) is safe only because it rides the order-version CAS (order-lifecycle.service.ts:256-257), a guard this endpoint does not have. Register: missing — D-51 (register :77, DB-10 in PHASE18_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md:133) covers settlements only.

**العلاج المقترح:** One migration: `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ledger_entries_one_reversal_per_order ON ledger_entries(order_id) WHERE entry_type IN ('order_returned','manual_adjustment')`, and map P2002 from :109 to the existing 409 ALREADY_REVERSED — the same collision-by-index idiom `awardLoyaltyPoints` already relies on (loyalty.service.ts:15-17, 304-310). Alternatively lock the completion row first (`SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the order_completed entry at :103) so the check at :105 serializes; the index is the honest one because the ledger, not the service, is the record.

### 16-20-67 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:52`

**الادّعاء:** Guest checkout answers three distinguishable results for a phone number before any cart or authentication exists, telling a stranger whether that number is a registered or disabled customer of a named store — the disclosure the login door was rebuilt to prevent

**الأثر المزعوم:** One unauthenticated POST per number reveals «this Iraqi mobile number has an account at this pharmacy/shop» (409 CUSTOMER_ACCOUNT_EXISTS «هذا الرقم مسجّل لدينا — سجّل الدخول لإتمام الطلب») or «the shop disabled this person» (403 CUSTOMER_DISABLED «تعذر إتمام الطلب بهذا الرقم — تواصل مع المتجر») versus «unknown» (proceeds to CART_EMPTY). A relative, employer or stalker learns a named person's relationship to a named merchant one number at a time; the ceiling is 40 numbers per IP per store per 10 minutes. Each unknown-number probe also inserts a fake «ضيف» row with attacker-chosen name/email into the merchant's customer directory, and a planted email blocks the real owner's later registration with it.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/http/routes/storefront.ts:200-207 — the route reaches guestCustomerFor at :207 straight after the limiter at :205 and body parse at :206, before the cart is even read (the merge and CART_EMPTY check are inside guestCheckout at guest-checkout.service.ts:84-87, called later at storefront.ts:210). phase1-backend/src/services/guest-checkout.service.ts:51 looks the phone up; :52 throws 409 CUSTOMER_ACCOUNT_EXISTS for an account row; :53 throws 403 CUSTOMER_DISABLED for a disabled row; :59 otherwise CREATES a customer row for the probed number with the caller's fullName/email; :61-63 names customers_store_email_present_uq, so an email planted on a fake row later answers EMAIL_TAKEN to its real owner. The only ceiling is storefront.ts:205 (limitStorefrontWriteWithAddressCeiling: 12 per guest cookie, 40 per IP); shared.ts:99-116 documents that the cookie-keyed bucket is chosen by the caller, so the 40 per IP per store is the real bound. The platform's own rule for this exact disclosure is stated at phase1-backend/src/auth/identity.ts:152-170 (ABSENT_IDENTITY_HASH: a store-scoped phone lookup «is a named person's relationship to a named merchant, disclosed to a stranger, one number at a time») and enforced at auth.service.ts:292-301 for login; the older registration door leaks the same bit (auth.service.ts:271 → errors.ts:206 DUPLICATE_CUSTOMER_PHONE) at 8 per IP (storefront.ts:117), and guest checkout adds a fifth-as-wide door plus the disabled-customer state.

**العلاج المقترح:** Make the three states indistinguishable to an unauthenticated caller: in guest-checkout.service.ts:52-53 answer account and disabled phones with the same generic refusal code and sentence, and move guestCustomerFor after the cart-empty check (or into the checkout transaction, storefront.service.ts:808) so a probe must carry a real cart and a refused checkout creates no customer row. Add the phone-probe case to the guest-checkout suite asserting identical status/code/message for account, disabled and unknown numbers.

### 16-20-68 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/password-reset.service.ts:251`

**الادّعاء:** A guest customer row (no password) is treated as an account by the password-reset door, and a consumed reset leaves it stranded as isGuest=true with a password

**الأثر المزعوم:** Two real people are hurt. (a) Any address a merchant hand-types into a manual customer row, or that a guest leaves at checkout, becomes a target for «استعادة كلمة السر» mail from the platform's sending identity for an account that never existed — precisely the state manual-provisioning.service.ts:100-103 says it removed. (b) A shopper who follows such a link, sets a password and signs in is still shown «ضيف» and «أنشئ كلمة مرور» in the storefront menu, and because the row is still a guest, `registerCustomer` will hand it — orders, addresses, loyalty points — to ANY registrant who types his phone number, silently overwriting the password he just set.

**الدليل المزعوم:** manual-provisioning.service.ts:127-141 creates `{ email, isGuest: true }` with no passwordHash (comment :100-103: the old random hash's «only thing they achieved was to make the row look like an account to the recovery flow»); guest-checkout.service.ts:59 creates `{ email, passwordHash: null, isGuest: true }`; prisma/schema.prisma:303 passwordHash is nullable, :310 isGuest. routes/storefront.ts:149-152 → requestPasswordReset({ kind: 'customer', email, storeId }). password-reset.service.ts:251-252 resolveIdentity(customer) = `findFirst({ where: { storeId, email, isActive: true } })` — no isGuest:false and no passwordHash-not-null filter — so :460-494 mints the token and queues the mail. Consume :594-595 writes ONLY passwordHash; isGuest is never flipped. auth.service.ts:289-299 loginCustomer never checks isGuest, so he signs in. Stranded state: client/src/components/StorefrontShell.tsx:178 renders «ضيف» and :188 «أنشئ كلمة مرور»; GuestPasswordCard.tsx:18 shows the guest card; auth.service.ts:260-267 registerCustomer finds `{ storeId, phoneNormalized, isGuest: true }` and updates with `...data` (which contains the registrant's passwordHash) — overwriting the reset-set password.

**العلاج المقترح:** In resolveIdentity's customer branch (password-reset.service.ts:251) add `isGuest: false, passwordHash: { not: null }` to the where (a guest row must not mint a token — the miss branch's constant-work decoy already covers the timing). Defensively, in consume (:595) for kind === 'customer' write `{ passwordHash, isGuest: false }` so a recovered row can never remain a claimable guest.

### 16-20-69 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/password-reset.service.ts:595`

**الادّعاء:** A merchant's password reset does not clear passwordSetByProvisionerAt, so after recovering his account he is still gated as if a platform actor held his password

**الأثر المزعوم:** The hand-recruited launch merchants (B-09) receive a temporary password over WhatsApp — the population most likely to press «نسيت كلمة السر». After the reset he signs in, is redirected to «الأمان», and every store screen answers 403 «كلمة المرور الحالية اختارها من أنشأ حسابك. غيّرها من «الأمان» لتتابع» — a sentence that is now false, since he chose the password himself a minute ago via his own mailbox. He must invent a SECOND new password (the same one is refused as PASSWORD_UNCHANGED) before he can see a single order. Confusing, wastes a support call, and misstates who knows his password.

Next login: auth.service.ts:190-197 `mustChangePassword = Boolean(merchant.passwordSetByProvisionerAt)` → redirectTo /merchant/dashboard/account/security. Every merchant surface: staff-permissions.ts:131-137 throws 403 PASSWORD_CHANGE_REQUIRED via shared.ts:239/245 and staff-permissions.ts:246. password.service.ts:96-98 refuses the just-set password as PASSWORD_UNCHANGED. Ledger docs/DECISIONS_LEDGER.md:323 (ق-218) states the flag marks «كلمة المرور التي يكتبها مُنشئ الحساب» — a mailbox-proven reset password is by definition not that.

**العلاج المقترح:** In consumePasswordReset's transaction (password-reset.service.ts:595), when `kind === 'merchant'` write `data: { passwordHash, passwordSetByProvisionerAt: null }` (mirroring password.service.ts:112-114), and add a pr1 test case: provision a merchant, reset via link, log in, assert no mustChangePassword and a 200 from a store route.

### 16-20-70 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/password-reset.service.ts:451`

**الادّعاء:** Password-reset request leaks account existence: when CODIQ_PUBLIC_BASE_URL is unset, a found account answers 'unavailable' while a miss answers 'queued', defeating the 19-د constant-work anti-enumeration on this endpoint

**الأثر المزعوم:** 19-د invested a rollback-decoy 'constant work for a miss' specifically so this public, unauthenticated endpoint returns an identical answer for an address that exists and one that does not. That invariant holds only when a link can always be built. If CODIQ_PUBLIC_BASE_URL is unset — a misconfiguration the codebase records as having actually occurred in production (environment-report.ts:94-99, 'unset in production') — a real dashboard identity (owner/merchant/staff/platform_staff) falls into the null-link branch and gets delivery:'unavailable', while a non-existent address takes the miss branch and gets delivery:'queued'. An attacker can then enumerate which emails are approved/active accounts one probe at a time, the exact oracle 19-د closed on the five login doors. It is bounded to the window where mail IS configured but the base URL is not (with mail unconfigured, step 2 returns 'unavailable' for everyone).

**الدليل المزعوم:** requestPasswordReset: after the platform-wide gate if(!isEmailProviderConfigured()) return {delivery:'unavailable'} (:439), a miss goes identity===null -> constantWorkForMiss(...) -> return {delivery:'queued'} (:446-449); a hit builds the link and, if it is null, returns {delivery:'unavailable'} (:451-458). resetLinkFor returns null for a dashboard identity precisely when publicUrl(...) is null, i.e. CODIQ_PUBLIC_BASE_URL unset (:279-288, dashboard branch at :281). So hit->'unavailable' vs miss->'queued' is a stable existence oracle. The constant-work rationale this breaks is documented at :330-360.

**العلاج المقترح:** Decide delivery availability from platform state that does not depend on the resolved identity — check publicUrl()/base-URL configuration up front (alongside the isEmailProviderConfigured gate at :439) and return 'unavailable' uniformly for every caller when no link could be built, so a hit and a miss remain indistinguishable. Longer term, make CODIQ_PUBLIC_BASE_URL a boot requirement in deployed environments (environment-report already flags it) so this window cannot exist.

### 16-20-71 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/product-catalog.service.ts:259`

**الادّعاء:** The merchant catalog's ?q= search caps matches at 200, discards the engine's hasMore, and re-sorts the survivors by createdAt, so a list that ends with nextCursor:null tells the merchant 'that is all of them' when it is not — the same defect the storefront search was fixed for in 20-2.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A clothing or cosmetics merchant with more than 200 products matching a common word («قميص», «عطر») pages through his own catalog search, reaches the end, and is told there is nothing more; the 201st-and-later matches exist but are unreachable from this screen and nothing says so (the palette reports `truncated`; this list does not). Because the 200 are then ordered newest-first, the engine's exact-first ranking is also thrown away, so the product he is looking for can sit on page 8 behind newer near-matches.

**الدليل المزعوم:** listProducts merges two engine pages capped at SEARCH_ID_CAP = 200 (product-catalog.service.ts:231, :251-257) and keeps only `.hits.map(hit => hit.id)` (:258) — the SearchPage.hasMore that mergeSearchPages returns (search.types.ts:94-108) is dropped. The ids become `id: { in: matchedIds }` inside a createdAt keyset (:261), the query orders by [{createdAt:'desc'},{id:'desc'}] (:266), and pageOf mints nextCursor only while rows remain within those 200 (:267-268). The storefront had exactly this defect and documents its own fix — «a shop with 300 shirts showed a shopper searching «قميص» the first 24 and told him that was all of them» — with a position cursor over a ranked list (storefront.service.ts:104-114, :397-404); the merchant catalog was not converted. list-envelope.guard.mjs cannot see it because the route does thread a nextCursor (:228), and pg6.arabic-search.integration.mjs asserts presence of a known product, never completeness over a large match set.

**العلاج المقترح:** Mirror the storefront: page the merchant search by position over the engine's ranked ids with its own cursor kind (as storefront.service.ts:397-404 does), or — smaller still — surface the discarded `hasMore` as a `truncated: true` facet on the envelope (the palette already does this) so a merchant is told the list is cut rather than complete.

### 16-20-72 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/product-catalog.service.ts:530`

**الادّعاء:** deleteVariant hard-deletes a variant that still holds stock with no closing movement, and inventory_movements.variant_id is ON DELETE SET NULL, so the product's stock trail keeps «+N» rows for units that no longer exist anywhere.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant who deletes the «كبير» variant while it still shows 7 units loses those 7 from every count instantly, and the audit trail he is told to trust («so the stock trail stays honest», reservation-expiry-job.ts:27-35) now shows +7 initial_stock and later adjustments attached to a null variant with no row saying where the units went. Any reconciliation of movements against shelf stock for that product is permanently off by the deleted variant's balance, and the merchant has no way to tell a data loss from a theft.

**الدليل المزعوم:** product-catalog.service.ts:517-531: :524 refuses only the default variant; :525-527 refuse on order_items/inventory_reservations; :528-529 refuse on cart_items; there is no check on `variant.stockQuantity > 0` and no inventoryMovement is written; :530 `prisma.productVariant.delete` hard-deletes. Schema: prisma/migrations/20260826120000_phase20a_product_variants/migration.sql:150-151 `inventory_movements_variant_fk ... ON DELETE SET NULL` (the comment at :146-147 calls this «history survives»), so the variant's rows stay under the product with variant_id NULL while the stock they describe vanishes. The trail those rows feed is inventory-movements.service.ts:39-58 (listed per product, variantId selected at :58).

**العلاج المقترح:** In deleteVariant, inside one transaction: refuse with 409 VARIANT_HAS_STOCK («أفرغ مخزون المتغير أولاً») when `stockQuantity > 0`, or write a closing `inventoryMovement` of `delta: -variant.stockQuantity, reason: "variant_deleted"` before the delete so the product's trail sums to what remains.

The dashboard then shows him a daysLeft he never had the chance to use (entitlement.service.ts:181-186). ### 16-20-75 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/registration.service.ts:136`

**الادّعاء:** decideMerchantApproval checks `pending` outside the transaction and updates unconditionally, so two concurrent decisions both succeed and the last writer silently decides

**الأثر المزعوم:** Two platform actors with registrations.approve acting on the same application at once (owner rejects, staff approves): both pass the pending check, both commit, the merchant's final approval_status and isActive are whichever write landed last, and platform_audit_events carries two 'registration_decided' rows with opposite decisions for the same merchant. In manual mode this is the one gate that decides who sells cash-on-delivery under the platform's name; a rejection can be overturned by a slower approve click nobody intended to win. Rare, but the fix is one line and the ALREADY_DECIDED refusal already exists.

**الدليل المزعوم:** registration.service.ts:131 `prisma.merchant.findUnique(... approvalStatus ...)` outside any transaction; :133 `if (merchant.approvalStatus !== 'pending') throw 409 ALREADY_DECIDED` decided on that stale read; :135 opens the transaction; :136 `tx.merchant.update({ where: { id: merchantId }, data: { approvalStatus, isActive: approve } })` with no status predicate — Prisma `update` by id always matches; :137 `tx.store.updateMany({ where: { merchantId }, data: { isActive: approve } })` likewise; :139-146 the audit event is written regardless. Contrast with the pattern the same codebase uses elsewhere: order-lifecycle.service.ts:256-257 `updateMany({ where: { id, storeId, version } })` + `if (result.count !== 1) throw 409`, and trial-expiry-scanner.ts:83-87 `updateMany({ where: { id, status: 'active' } })` + `if (closed.count === 0) return`.

**العلاج المقترح:** Inside the transaction replace :136 with `const flipped = await tx.merchant.updateMany({ where: { id: merchantId, approvalStatus: 'pending' }, data: { approvalStatus: approve ? 'approved' : 'rejected', isActive: approve } }); if (flipped.count !== 1) throw new HttpError(409, 'ALREADY_DECIDED', 'طلب هذا التاجر محسوم بالفعل.');` so the store update and the audit row only follow the write that actually won.

### 16-20-76 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/audit-log.service.ts:118`

**الادّعاء:** The store activity log is gated by `catalog.audit.view` alone and returns staff emails and blocked customers' phone numbers to a reader who lacks `team.manage` / `customers.view`, the looser-door pattern ح-3 closed for the trash bin

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant who gives a staffer «سجل النشاط» (an allow override on `catalog.audit.view`) while deliberately leaving him without «العملاء» and «الفريق» — the exact configuration the codebase's own ع-2 and ح-3 notes treat as a real merchant choice — hands that staffer every colleague's email address, role and permission overrides (from `staff_membership` events) and the full phone number of every shopper the store has blocked (from `customer.blocked` events). In a cash-on-delivery market the phone IS the customer's identity, and a blocked shopper is precisely the person whose number should be visible to the fewest people. The dashboard renders the value verbatim.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Route guard is the single code: phase1-backend/src/http/routes/trash.ts:68-71 (`requireStoreActor(request, "catalog.audit.view")`, permissions never passed on). `listAuditEvents` takes no permission set and returns `entitySecondary` and the full `metadata` for every entity type: audit-log.service.ts:97-143 (projection at :130-141, no per-type filter in the `where` at :118-128). What is written into those fields: staff email as `entitySecondary` plus `metadata.email` / `overrides` at soft-delete.service.ts:274-289; blocked customer's phone as `entitySecondary` at customer-directory.service.ts:135-144. The contrasting, stricter rule the same module applies to the bin: `TRASH_GOVERNING_PERMISSION` / `mayTouchTrashEntity` at soft-delete.service.ts:619-636 and the `wants()` filter at :764-765 that hides staff rows from a reader without `team.manage`. Reachability: `store_staff` lacks all three codes by matrix (staff-permissions.ts:43-46) but overrides may grant any code (`assertPermissionCode` accepts the full list, :48-53; `resolvePermissionSet` applies `allow`, :63-73) through PUT …/team/:membershipId/overrides (routes/team.ts:33-36). Client shows the field: client/src/components/ActivityLog.tsx:192 (`event.entitySecondary`).

**العلاج المقترح:** Pass the reader's `permissions` from the route into `listAuditEvents` and apply the same governing map the bin uses: exclude `entityType: "staff_membership"` rows unless `team.manage` is held and `entityType: "customer"` rows unless `customers.view` is held (add `entityType: { notIn: [...] }` to the `where`), or, if the merchant wants the timeline intact, keep the rows but null `entitySecondary` and `metadata` for those types. Reuse `mayTouchTrashEntity`-style logic rather than a second table so the two surfaces cannot drift.

### 16-20-77 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/soft-delete/soft-delete.service.ts:144`

**الادّعاء:** softDeleteProduct's PRODUCT_HAS_ACTIVE_RESERVATION guard is an unlocked count that a concurrent Serializable checkout slips past, so a product can be trashed while a live order holds an active reservation on it

**الأثر المزعوم:** The guard promises «هذا المنتج عليه حجز مخزون نشط لطلب قائم — لا يمكن إزالته الآن» but a checkout that is mid-transaction at the moment the merchant taps delete commits its reservation AFTER the count and BEFORE the product row is stamped, leaving a trashed product with a `new` order on it. The order itself still flows (completion decrements the variant with no deleted_at filter), but when the COD delivery is refused at the door — the normal return here — the inspection's restock path refuses with VARIANT_TRASHED (return-inspection.service.ts:183-190) until the merchant restores a product he deliberately removed, and the row is purge-blocked for good by the reservation's RESTRICT key. Small, recoverable, but it is exactly the read-then-write the file already admits for the cart guard (:133-139) and does not admit for this one.

**الدليل المزعوم:** 1. Checkout runs Serializable and locks the variant AND its product row `FOR UPDATE OF v, p` (storefront.service.ts:899-908), then inserts the active reservation at :1001 and commits at :1008; the lock is held for the whole transaction. 2. softDeleteProduct runs in a default READ COMMITTED transaction (soft-delete.service.ts:121) and reads the product with a plain findFirst (:122-125, no lock), counts live-cart items (:140) and active reservations (:144) — both plain SELECTs that see nothing of the uncommitted checkout — and only then issues `tx.product.update` (:172), which blocks on the product row lock held by the checkout. 3. When the checkout commits, the blocked UPDATE re-evaluates its `WHERE id = product.id`, still true, and proceeds: deletedAt is set and isActive false (:172-174) with the new reservation already `active` and the order already `new`. Nothing re-checks the counts after acquiring the lock. (The reverse interleaving is safe: the checkout's lock query filters `deleted_at IS NULL` at :905 and a committed delete surfaces as PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE at :927.) 4. Downstream: return inspection restock on that variant throws VARIANT_TRASHED (return-inspection.service.ts:183-190); purge is refused by `inventory_reservations.product_id … onDelete: Restrict` (schema.prisma:918) and stamped kept-for-records (purge-job.ts:186-188, 207-209).

**العلاج المقترح:** Take the product row lock before the two counts, using the idiom the file already has for restore: `SELECT id FROM products WHERE id = $id::uuid AND store_id = $storeId::uuid AND deleted_at IS NULL FOR UPDATE` (cf. lockTrashedRow at :337-343) as the first statement of softDeleteProduct. A checkout holding `FOR UPDATE OF … p` then serialises ahead of the delete and the counts at :140/:144 see its reservation; a delete that wins the lock makes the Serializable checkout's own FOR UPDATE fail with 40001, retry, and land on PRODUCT_UNAVAILABLE.

### 16-20-78 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:246`

**الادّعاء:** The rate-limit refusal reaches merchants, staff, the owner and everyone on the reset pages in English because the server message wins over the Arabic fallback

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant or store staffer who hits the 30/10-min shared-IP bucket on the dashboard door, or anyone who hits the reset ceilings (20/h per IP on request, 30/10-min per IP on consume), reads 'Too many requests. Please try again later.' on an Arabic-first screen with no hint of how long to wait. The Arabic sentence written for exactly this case in DashboardLogin is dead code.

**الدليل المزعوم:** phase1-backend/src/services/storefront.service.ts:246 throws HttpError(429,'RATE_LIMITED','Too many requests. Please try again later.'); phase1-backend/src/http/errors.ts:130 passes HttpError messages through unchanged. client/src/pages/DashboardLogin.tsx:131 `payload?.error?.message ?? FALLBACK_MESSAGES[code]` — message first, so the Arabic at :78 is never used. client/src/pages/PasswordResetRequest.tsx:79 and client/src/pages/PasswordResetConsume.tsx:85 print `payload?.error?.message` verbatim. Ceilings that raise it on those pages: phase1-backend/src/services/password-reset.service.ts:423-434 and :552-564; dashboard doors phase1-backend/src/http/routes/auth.ts:23,46,81,94. (The storefront client is unaffected: client/src/lib/storefront-api.ts:349 maps by code first.)

**العلاج المقترح:** Change the one string at storefront.service.ts:246 to Arabic, e.g. «محاولات كثيرة خلال وقت قصير — انتظر بضع دقائق ثم أعد المحاولة.», and include the window so the person knows how long; optionally make DashboardLogin.tsx:131 prefer FALLBACK_MESSAGES[code] for RATE_LIMITED.

### 16-20-79 · منخفضة · `phase1-backend/tests/neon-connection.test.ts:32`

**الادّعاء:** `neon-connection.test.ts` still inserts a `platform_owners` row into whatever database `CODIQ_POSTGRES_URL` names, with no test-database guard, and the register has no row for it although the audit rated it High (CI-2).

**الأثر المزعوم:** Any developer who runs `npx vitest -c vitest.backend.config.ts` in a shell where the production URL is exported (the state a shell is in right after a manual production migration) writes and deletes an owner row in the live identities table; a failure between the create and the delete leaves a second `platform_owners` row, which falsifies the "one active owner row" figure PROJECT_STATE.md:55 audits as a live security metric. Rare, but it is exactly the class of trap the register exists to name, and it was dropped between the audit and the register.

**الدليل المزعوم:** neon-connection.test.ts:16 and :28 read `process.env.CODIQ_POSTGRES_URL` unfiltered; :32 `client.platformOwner.create(...)`, :35 `delete`. vitest.backend.config.ts:4-7 includes `phase1-backend/tests/**/*.test.ts` with no `setupFiles` and no guard; vitest is installed (package.json:240); no package.json script and no .github/workflows/ci.yml step invokes vitest (grep returns only the devDependency line), so nothing exercises or guards it. docs/history/reports/PHASE18_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md:209 (CI-2) names this file and the trap as High; docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md carries no D-row referencing vitest or neon-connection (grep finds none).

**العلاج المقترح:** Register it (Low, S). Then delete the file, or add `setupFiles: ["phase1-backend/tests/test-database-guard.ts"]` to vitest.backend.config.ts so the same marker check the integration suites use runs before any `*.test.ts` touches a database.

### 16-20-80 · منخفضة · `docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:78`

**الادّعاء:** D-52 is mis-stated by a factor of 4.5: there are 27 hand-rolled `api<T>` copies in the client, not 6, and they already surface different error text for the same server failure.

**الأثر المزعوم:** A merchant hitting the same 401/403/429 sees a different sentence depending on which panel he is on (the till throws one mapping, the finance centre another, several GET-only copies cannot even send a body), which is the raw-code experience D-53 complains about; and the row's effort "M" and Phase-21 slot were sized for six copies, so the cross-cutting changes the register itself plans — a per-identity cookie for D-88, a CSRF header, a 401 redirect — now cost 27 edits each, and the register does not know it.

**الدليل المزعوم:** Register line 78 says "6 نسخ متطابقة من `api<T>`" and line 168 says the unified client "ما يزال غير موجود في `client/src/lib/`". Grep for `async function api<T>` in client/src returns 27 definitions (TillPage.tsx:91, UpgradeRequestsPanel.tsx:30, MerchantAnalytics.tsx:43, MarketingIntegrationsPanel.tsx:23, LoyaltySettingsCard.tsx:37, InventoryMovementsPanel.tsx:34, FinanceCenter.tsx:19, EmailComplaintsSection.tsx:17, DeliveryRatesManager.tsx:19, DashboardOverview.tsx:21, CustomersDirectory.tsx:26, CouponsManager.tsx:44, SubscriptionsManager.tsx:22, StoreTeamManager.tsx:55, StoreMessagingCapsPanel.tsx:20, AbandonedCartsPanel.tsx:26, PlatformTeamManager.tsx:80, PlatformStoresDirectory.tsx:22, PlatformSettingsPanel.tsx:12, PlansManager.tsx:33, ReturnInspectionHistory.tsx:41, OwnerAccountPanels.tsx:35, RegistrationCenter.tsx:12, OrderWorkspace.tsx:116, NotificationCenter.tsx:113, ProductCatalogManager.tsx:57, MerchantsDirectory.tsx:25); client/src/lib contains only storefront-api.ts and no api-client (glob). Divergence read: client/src/pages/TillPage.tsx:91-96 throws `new Error(apiError(payload))`; client/src/components/FinanceCenter.tsx:19-24 throws `body?.error?.message ?? "فشل الطلب (status)"` and forces `content-type: application/json` on every call including GET; DashboardOverview.tsx:21 and MerchantAnalytics.tsx:43 accept no `init` at all.

**العلاج المقترح:** Correct the row: count 27 (with the grep pattern recorded so the next audit can re-measure), effort L not M, and add the concrete symptom (divergent error text per panel) to the impact column so D-53 and D-88 planning can cite it.

### 16-20-81 · منخفضة · `docs/history/reports/PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:166`

**الادّعاء:** Register lists D-40 (NotificationSettings.apiKey stored as plaintext) as still-open, but the column is now encrypted at rest via the secret-box, so the register overstates live security debt

**الأثر المزعوم:** A launch/acceptance gate run off this register (the register itself warns the phase-26 gate is driven by it) will treat an at-rest-secret exposure as still-open and either re-do the work or hold a gate on a resolved item. Harm is only wasted effort / false debt (the safe direction), but it is exactly the kind of stale row the register's own preface says to verify against code.

**الدليل المزعوم:** PHASE18_DEBT_REGISTER.md:166 lists under the Phase-19 open bucket: "D-40 (`apiKey` نصاً خاماً)" and the top table row D-40 is not marked ✅. But phase1-backend/src/services/notification-settings.service.ts:13,29-43 seals and opens the value through auth/secret-box.ts (sealSecret/openSecret), setNotificationApiKey writes only sealed bytes, and the file's own header comment is titled "19-د (D-40)" describing the fix. The column also has no live writer yet (store-bootstrap creates apiKey:null), so there is no plaintext at rest today.

**العلاج المقترح:** Move D-40 to the closed/partially-closed section with its evidence (notification-settings.service.ts:39-43 + auth/secret-box.ts), noting it is sealed-at-rest ahead of the SMS integration that will first write it.
